# Changes

## 2026-10-22
- Change (shot): OPUSDM-2, the numeric "Scale (%)" field of the OpusShot editor is now a "Scale" drop-down with fixed choices (400%, 200%, 150%, 100%, 75%, 50%, 25%), 100% selected at start. The saved image is scaled by the chosen percentage as before (`export::scale`). New `SCALE_PERCENTS`, `DEFAULT_SCALE_INDEX`, `scale_percent` in `editor.rs`. Tests: 1 unit test on the choices; all 59 shot tests pass, clippy clean for shot. Checked on screen (debug build, running X11 session): the drop-down with the 7 choices, 50% + Quick save gave a PNG half the size of the selected area. NOT checked: Save as and Copy with a scale other than 100%, Wayland, the installed binary (`live-update.sh`), the CLAUDE.md rule 4 checklist (no lister/hub/ipc/ui change).
- Feature (panel): OPUSDM-3, the application menu is wider (520 px, was 260), has a "Filter applications" search box on top that narrows the apps by name (case-insensitive substring, folders open while filtering), hides folders with no apps, and has a footer apart from the list: "About" on the left, Logout and Shutdown on the right. New `appmenu::matches_filter`, `MENU_WIDTH`. Tests: 2 unit tests on the name filter; clippy clean for the panel. Checked on screen (release build, Xephyr sandbox): the new layout and width, the footer, the empty filter box. NOT checked: **typing in the filter on X11 does not work**: the panel is a dock window that the window manager does not focus, so the keys go to the previously focused window (also after a click in the box); a fix would activate the panel window while the menu is open and restore the previous focus on close, not done. Not checked either: the filter on Wayland (layer-shell keyboard mode OnDemand), Enter in the filter, the installed session (`live-update.sh`), the CLAUDE.md rule 4 checklist (Lister, global hotkeys, hub).
- Change (release): workspace version bumped from 0.11.0 to 0.12.0 (`Cargo.toml`, `Cargo.lock`); every crate inherits it. No code changed. Checked: `cargo update --workspace --offline` only touches the workspace crates. NOT checked: a full build and a release with the new number (no tag or package made).

## 2026-10-21
- Fix (scripts): `live-update.sh` now installs the folder handler too, as `install.sh` does: the `/usr/local/bin/opusdm-open-folder` link, `/usr/share/applications/opusdm-lister.desktop` and `update-desktop-database`. An install kept up to date only with `live-update.sh` never got the entry, so the hub logged `opusdm-lister.desktop is not installed` at every start, `inode/directory` stayed `org.kde.dolphin.desktop` and `xdg-open <folder>` (for example "Open Folder" in Claude Desktop Settings) started a Dolphin that showed no window. Checked: `sh -n`, `--help`; on this machine the owner copied the two files by hand and restarted the hub, which logged `folders now open in a Lister (was Some("org.kde.dolphin.desktop"))` and `xdg-mime query default inode/directory` gives `opusdm-lister.desktop`. NOT checked: a full `live-update.sh` run with the new lines (needs sudo), `xdg-open` of a folder and "Open Folder" in Claude Desktop opening a Lister, the CLAUDE.md rule 4 checklist (no app code changed).
- Feature (viewer): the Markdown view shows the pictures of raw HTML `<img src=... alt=... width=...>` tags (block or inline, quotes or not, entities decoded), as in the README files that center a logo with `<p align="center"><img ...></p>`; the rest of the HTML is still dropped and an `<img>` in a table cell stays `[image: alt]`. A whole-number `width` (or `600px`) sets the drawn width (up or down, never wider than 800 px, proportions kept). Pictures at an `http(s)://` address (Markdown `![]()` or `<img>`) are now downloaded with `curl` on a worker (`--fail --location`, http/https only also on redirects, 20 s, 16 MiB); a failed download is logged with `err_log!` and shows `[image: alt]`. Other addresses (`file://`, ...) are unchanged. `curl` added to the `.deb` Recommends. New `markdown::_html_images`, `Run::image_width`, `markdown_view::_download`. Tests: 6 parser tests (HTML block with width, inline, block without pictures, table cell, attribute parsing, unclosed tag), `an_html_width_sets_the_size_up_to_the_widest`, `only_http_addresses_are_downloaded`, `a_failed_download_is_an_error_with_a_message`; all 148 viewer tests pass, clippy has only the old `image_view.rs` warning. Checked on screen (debug build, running X11 session): `renfield/README.md` shows its logo (webp, width 600) and the second picture; a test file showed a web PNG, a shields.io SVG badge, an inline `<img width=64>` and, for an unreachable address, the `[image: broken]` text plus the log line. NOT checked: the installed `/opt/opusdm` binary (not updated), many pictures on a slow network, the CLAUDE.md rule 4 checklist (no lister/hub/ipc/ui change).
- Change (scripts): the `.deb` of `build-deb.sh` links every `opusdm-*` command into `/usr/bin` (it linked only `opusdm-open-folder`), so the commands are on the PATH of any session, as in the dreamos-ubuntu ISO. The files stay in `/opt/opusdm/bin`; the hub finds `opusdm-lister` next to itself through `current_exe`, which resolves the link. Checked: `dpkg-deb -c` lists the 16 links. NOT checked: starting the hub through the `/usr/bin` link (the ISO build will exercise it), a release with the new package.
- Docs (ci): first real release checked. Tag `v0.11.0` ran `release-deb.yml`: the `deb` and `apt-repo` jobs passed, the Release has `opusdm_0.11.0_amd64.deb`, `gh-pages` has the signed repository, and with Pages on, a clean `ubuntu:26.04` container added the repository with the lines of `index.html`, `apt-get update` verified the signature and `apt-get install opusdm` installed 0.11.0 (no missing libraries, the three sessions registered). The CI `.deb` needs glibc >= 2.43 (built on 26.04). NOT checked: a second release over the existing `gh-pages` (pruning, `apt upgrade`), Ubuntu 26.10, logging into an OpusDM session from the installed package.
- Feature (scripts, ci): **apt repository on GitHub Pages**. New `scripts/build-apt-repo.sh <repo dir> <deb>...` (needs `apt-utils`, `gpg`, `GPG_KEY_ID`) builds `pool/`, `dists/stable/main/binary-<arch>/Packages`, a signed `Release`/`InRelease`/`Release.gpg`, the public key `opusdm.gpg` and an `index.html` with the install lines; only the last 5 versions stay in the pool. `release-deb.yml` has a new `apt-repo` job (tags only, after the `deb` job): it reads the current `gh-pages` content, runs the script with the secret `APT_GPG_PRIVATE_KEY` (armored secret key, no passphrase) and force-pushes one orphan commit to `gh-pages`, so the history does not keep old `.deb` files. One time setup, by the owner: create the GPG key, add the secret, enable Pages on the `gh-pages` branch. Users: `deb [signed-by=/etc/apt/keyrings/opusdm.gpg] https://fsoft72.github.io/opusdm stable main`. Checked: in a clean `ubuntu:26.04` container with a throwaway key, the script output served over HTTP, `apt-get update` (signature verified) and `apt-get install opusdm` ok; the workflow file parses as YAML. NOT checked: the `apt-repo` job on GitHub (never run), the real key and Pages, a second release over an existing `gh-pages` (pruning and upgrade), an `apt upgrade` from an older version.
- Feature (scripts): new `scripts/release.sh [-y]` tags the current commit `v<Cargo.toml version>` (annotated) and pushes the branch, then the tag, to `origin`; the tag starts `release-deb.yml`. It refuses a detached HEAD, a dirty working tree and a tag that already exists locally or on the remote, and asks for confirmation unless `-y`. Checked: syntax, `--help`, and that it stops on a dirty tree. NOT checked: a real tag and push (never run, it publishes), the workflow it triggers.
- Feature (scripts, ci): **`.deb` package**. New `scripts/build-deb.sh [--skip-build]` builds `target/deb/opusdm_<version>_<arch>.deb` from the `dist/` of `build-release.sh` (it runs it unless `--skip-build`). The layout is the one of `install.sh --x11 --wayland labwc --wayland wayfire` with the same prefix: `/opt/opusdm`, the three session entries, the Openbox theme, the icon and desktop entries in `/usr/share`, and a `/usr/bin/opusdm-open-folder` link. Binaries are stripped in the staging copy only. `Depends` comes from `dpkg-shlibdeps` (so it follows the Ubuntu release the package is built on), the desktop pieces (openbox, picom, labwc, wayfire, wlr-randr, grim, gstreamer plugins...) are `Recommends`. New workflow `.github/workflows/release-deb.yml`: on a `v*` tag it builds in an `ubuntu:26.04` container, checks the tag against the `Cargo.toml` version and attaches the `.deb` to the GitHub Release (`workflow_dispatch` only keeps it as a run artifact). No app code changed. Checked: the script run on this machine, `dpkg-deb -I/-c` (files, modes, depends), install with `apt` and removal with `dpkg -r` in a clean `ubuntu:26.04` container (no missing libraries with `ldd`, `/opt/opusdm` removed). NOT checked: the GitHub workflow (never run), logging into the OpusDM sessions from an installed `.deb`, an upgrade over a `live-update.sh` / `install.sh` install (same `/opt/opusdm` paths: the files of both would overlap), a PPA (not done: Launchpad needs source packages and vendored crates).

## 2026-10-19
- Feature (shot): "Scale (%)" spin button in the bottom bar of the editor (1 to 400, default 100). Quick save and Save as write the image (after the crop) scaled by that percentage with the same factor on both sides, so the aspect ratio is kept (Lanczos3, a side never goes under 1 px); at 100% the image is untouched. Copy to the clipboard is NOT scaled. The value is not persisted. New `export::scaled_size` / `export::scale` (`image_to_save` in the editor). Tests: `scaled_size_keeps_the_ratio_and_never_reaches_zero`, `scale_resizes_and_a_full_scale_is_the_same_image`; all opusdm-shot tests pass, clippy clean. NOT checked on screen: the new spin button, a saved scaled file, the CLAUDE.md rule 4 checklist (no lister/hub/ipc/ui change).
- Fix (polkit): `opusdm-polkit` talks to `polkit-agent-helper-1` through the systemd socket `/run/polkit/agent-helper.socket` when it exists (polkit 126+, Ubuntu 26.10), like libpolkit-agent: it writes the user and the cookie, then the same line protocol runs on the socket. There the helper is no longer setuid, and starting it directly failed with "Incorrect permissions on /usr/lib/polkit-1/polkit-agent-helper-1 (needs to be setuid root)" (every pkexec from the installed dreamos-ubuntu). No socket, or a socket that refuses the connection (logged with `err_log!`), still starts the setuid helper as before. New `Session::open` / `Session::connect`; `Session` keeps either the child or the stream, and dropping a socket session shuts the stream down (the helper ends on EOF). The helper tests now serialize writing and starting the fake helper scripts (`EXEC_LOCK`): a parallel fork could inherit the write descriptor and the exec failed with ETXTBSY. Tests: `the_socket_helper_gets_user_cookie_and_password`, `open_prefers_the_socket_over_the_helper`, `open_starts_the_helper_without_a_socket`, `open_falls_back_to_the_helper_when_the_socket_refuses`; 10 runs of the crate tests green, clippy clean. Checked in the installed dreamos-ubuntu VM (UEFI, polkit socket active): DreamOS Installer ran its pkexec install, the dialog took the password and the journal shows `polkit-agent-helper@...-1813_...-1000.service` (the socket helper for opusdm-polkit, uid 1000) finishing successfully. NOT checked: a wrong password against the real socket helper, the running session (host polkitd 126-2ubuntu0.1 has no socket, so it keeps the setuid path), the CLAUDE.md rule 4 checklist (no lister/hub/ipc/ui change).
- Change (hub): a double click (or Open) on a `.desktop` file of the desktop asks first: a requester `Launch "<Name>"?` with the command line it runs as the detail, buttons Launch (Return) and Cancel (Esc). `open_file` takes the parent window (`widgets.primary_window()`, as Delete does). Checked: Xvfb session in an ubuntu:26.10 container with xdotool: the double click showed the requester and ran nothing, Esc ran nothing, a second double click plus Return ran the entry once. NOT checked: the context menu Open, the running session, the CLAUDE.md rule 4 checklist.
- Feature (iconview, hub): the desktop shows a `.desktop` file as the application it launches: its `Name=` (translated by GIO) as the label and its `Icon=` from the icon theme, instead of the file name and the generic file icon (the live ISO installer launcher showed as `lubuntu-calamares.desktop`). New `IconField::set_show_desktop_entries`, off by default and turned on only by the hub desktop field, so the Lister and the group windows are unchanged; `IconItem::show_desktop_entry` does the work for every item `sync_realized` builds. A file GIO cannot parse (GIO also rejects an `Exec=` program that is not installed) and an `Icon=` the theme lacks keep the old look. The rename entry still edits the file name. Tests: GTK scenarios `icon_item_desktop_entry`, `field_desktop_entries` (checked they run on a display). Checked: Xvfb session in an ubuntu:26.10 container, opusdm-hub desktop with two launchers: labels "Install DreamOS" and "Launch Test" with their theme icons. NOT checked: the running session, HiDPI, a rename of a launcher, the CLAUDE.md rule 4 checklist.
- Fix (hub): a double click (or Open in the context menu) on a `.desktop` file of the desktop launches it, as a group member already did, instead of opening it as text. `open_file` classifies the file with `leftout::launch_action` and launches `DesktopEntry` files with `gio::DesktopAppInfo` (`launch_desktop_file`), before the file types: the default "Source file" type opens `*.desktop` in `opusdm-viewer`, which stays the behaviour in the Lister. A file GIO cannot parse is logged and opened with the default application. Other files are unchanged (file type, else default application). Checked: Xvfb session in an ubuntu:26.10 container, Openbox + opusdm-hub, xdotool double click: `~/Desktop/launch-test.desktop` (`Exec=touch /tmp/launched`) ran and no viewer opened; `~/Desktop/notes.txt` still opened `opusdm-viewer`. NOT checked: the context menu Open, an invalid `.desktop`, the running session, the CLAUDE.md rule 4 checklist.
- Fix (ui): dark mode on GTK 4.20+. `apply_theme_mode` also sets `gtk-interface-color-scheme` (`dark`/`light`), the GtkSettings property GTK 4.20+ uses to pick the theme variant; it ignores the deprecated `gtk-application-prefer-dark-theme`, so on GTK 4.24 (Ubuntu 26.10) the GTK-drawn buttons of the panel (clock, notifications, trays) were light. The property is looked up at run time (`find_property`): older GTK lacks it and nothing changes there; an unexpected type or nick is logged with `err_log!`. Written only when it differs, like the other two settings. Test: `color_scheme_nick_follows_the_theme_mode`. Checked: Xvfb session in an ubuntu:26.10 container with the dreamos-ubuntu packages (GTK 4.24.1), Openbox + opusdm-hub, skel `gtk-4.0/settings.ini` without any color-scheme key: before the fix the clock and bell buttons were light, after it they are dark. NOT checked: switching to the light theme from the settings, the running session (GTK 4.20.1), the CLAUDE.md rule 4 checklist.
- Chore: added the MIT `LICENSE` file at the root (Copyright (c) 2026 Fabio Rotondo); `license = "MIT"` in `[workspace.package]`, inherited by every crate and xtask (`license.workspace = true`). Metadata only, no code change. Checked: `cargo metadata`.

## 2026-10-18
- Config (openbox): removed the `Print` and `Alt+Print` keybindings from `openbox-rc.xml`. They ran `scrot`, which is not installed ("Failed to execute child process scrot"). Screenshots are handled by OpusDM only (`Ctrl+Print` global hotkey of the hub). Checked: `xmllint` on the file. NOT done: the installed copy `/opt/opusdm/config/openbox-rc.xml` (needs sudo) and the reload of the running Openbox.
- Feature (shot): Ctrl+Enter, or a click of the right button, while marking the area keeps the whole screen (selection or not): the overlay passes the full capture to the editor like Enter does for a selection. The label of the overlay says so. New Crop tool in the editor tool bar: drag the area to keep, drag its sides to adjust, a click without a drag removes it; the part outside is dimmed and the saved/copied image is the rendered picture cropped to it (`selection::crop_rect`, `final_image`). The crop is non-destructive: the canvas keeps the full image and annotations stay editable outside the crop; there is no undo step for it (click to remove). Tests: `a_crop_is_a_rect_or_nothing`, the crop icon in `every_icon_paints_something`; all opusdm-shot tests pass, clippy clean. NOT checked on screen: the overlay shortcuts, the Crop tool, a saved cropped file, Wayland, the CLAUDE.md rule 4 checklist (the global Print hotkey that starts opusdm-shot was not exercised).
- Change (ui, panel, hub): the About OpusDM window shows the logo `assets/opusdm-full.png` (embedded, 320x105) at the top, in place of the big "OpusDM" text. The two copies of the dialog (panel start menu, hub desktop menu) now call one `opusdm_ui::show_about` (`ui/src/about.rs`); the requester got a private `build_with_header` / `requester_rich_header` for the picture. A logo that cannot be decoded is logged with `err_log!` and the window opens without it. No ownership or event change (same requester, same OK button). Checked: `cargo build` and `cargo clippy` of ui, panel, hub. NOT checked: the window on screen (neither from the panel menu nor from the desktop menu), the CLAUDE.md rule 4 checklist.
- Change (panel): the start menu button picture fills the bar. The picture is 36 px (was 18, bar is 40) and the button has class `opusdm-panel-menu` with `padding: 0`, so it no longer sits inside a padded button. Only CSS and a size constant, no ownership or event change. Checked: `cargo build -p opusdm-panel`. The button look is removed too (no background, border or shadow, also on hover, pressed and open). The button look is gone: the rule targets `menubutton.opusdm-panel-menu > button`, the inner button of the MenuButton (a first try on `button.opusdm-panel-menu` matched nothing). Checked in Xephyr (1280x800, scratch config): flat icon, no frame. NOT checked: the running session, hover/pressed look, a custom menu icon, a bottom panel, the CLAUDE.md rule 4 checklist.
- Feature (theme editor, panel): new "Hub" tab in the Theme Editor. Each part is optional ("Set by this theme") and saved in `theme.json` under `hub`: the hub bar paint (flat colour, gradient with angle, or picture with fit), the start menu button picture (png or svg, replaces the built-in `ball.png`) and the font family and size of the start menu once opened. `opusdm_ipc::look` gets `HubLook`, `HubBar`, `ThemeHub`; `Settings.hub` is a look field resolved from the active theme (`resolve_look`, `resolve_draft`, `same_look`), so the live preview, Apply and Save theme carry it. The hub sends `HubEvent::HubLookChanged` and the panel (`panel/src/hub_look.rs`) swaps a second stylesheet over the panel one and the picture of every start menu button alive; the menu popover has the `opusdm-appmenu` class, the target of the font rule. Colours are checked as hex colours before they reach CSS, picture names must be bare file names (like the surfaces). Theme import keeps `.svg` files in `backgrounds/`. Tests: ipc, writer, apply, importer, panel CSS builder; all workspace tests pass. Checked in the Xephyr sandbox (`run-x11.sh`, config with a gradient bar): the bar is painted with the gradient. NOT verified: the Theme Editor tab itself and its live preview (not opened), a picture bar, a custom menu icon (the test icon was a copy of ball.png), the menu font on the opened popover, the checks of CLAUDE.md rule 4 (Lister, global hotkeys, hub roles) and the running session.
- Fix (scripts): `run-x11.sh` can be started from any folder. It used `$(pwd)` for the Openbox and picom configs, the release binaries and `cargo build`, so it only worked from the repository root. Now it goes to its own folder (`readlink -f` of the script, so a symlink works too) right after reading the arguments; a relative `--config-dir` is resolved before that, against the folder the user started it from. Checked: from the scratchpad folder with `--config-dir cfg2 :79` the sandbox built, used the repo's openbox-rc.xml / picom.conf, ran the hub from the repository folder on :79 and filled `cfg2`; a missing relative folder fails with a message; the real hub kept running and the private config folder was removed on exit. Not checked: `XEPHYR_OB_CONFIG` / `XEPHYR_PICOM_CONFIG` given as relative paths (they now count from the repository folder).
- Feature (scripts): `run-x11.sh --config-dir DIR` (or `XEPHYR_CONFIG_DIR`) runs the sandbox on the OpusDM configuration in DIR instead of `~/.config/opusdm`. OpusDM reads `$XDG_CONFIG_HOME/opusdm` (`ipc::settings::config_dir`), so the script makes a private `XDG_CONFIG_HOME` (`mktemp`) whose `opusdm` entry is a symlink to DIR; every sandbox process inherits it, so `~/.config/mimeapps.list` is not touched either and picom.conf is copied into DIR. DIR must exist (a typo would otherwise silently give defaults); the private folder is removed on exit (only the symlink, DIR stays). Only the config is redirected: `HOME`, `XDG_STATE_HOME`, `XDG_DATA_HOME` (trash) and the thumbnail cache are still the user's. Checked in Xephyr :79 with an empty DIR: the sandbox hub had `XDG_CONFIG_HOME=/tmp/opusdm-xephyr-config-*`, the hub wrote dock/filetypes/functions/hotkeys/menu/picom.conf and themes into DIR, `~/.config/opusdm/settings.json` mtime unchanged, exit removed the private folder and left DIR, the real hub (DISPLAY=:0) kept running; a missing DIR and `--config-dir` without a value fail with a message. Not checked: a DIR copied from a full real config, the Lister/hotkey checklist inside that sandbox.
- Docs (scripts): `run-x11.sh` already started Xephyr at 1920x1080 by default (`RESOLUTION="${2:-${XEPHYR_RES:-1920x1080}}"`); only the usage comment still said 1280x800 and is fixed. No behaviour change. Checked: Xephyr started with the same `-screen 1920x1080` flags reports `dimensions: 1920x1080`. The full script (hub, openbox, picom) was not run.
- Feature (scripts): `live-update.sh --no-restart` builds and replaces the binaries in PREFIX/bin (rename install, as before) and then stops: no OpusDM process is stopped or started, running ones keep the old code, new launches use the new one. Combined with `--all` it is refused (exit 2). `--config` still reloads the window manager. Help range (`sed -n '2,29p'`) widened for the longer header. Checked: `sh -n`, `-h` output, the `--all --no-restart` refusal. NOT run end to end: the build and the sudo copy into /opt/opusdm were not executed here.
- Feature (viewer): a picture with an alpha channel is shown over a grey checkerboard (8 px squares) instead of the black stage. `ViewPaintable::new` takes `checkered` (the image `has_alpha`); `snapshot_checkers` draws one 2x2 tile with `push_repeat` behind the texture, only inside the picture rectangle, so the black stage around it stays. Checked in Xephyr with a 64x64 RGBA PNG (red disc on transparent): the corners show the checkerboard, zoom label and stage unchanged. NOT checked: zoomed-in scrolling, rotation, PDF pages (they have alpha but an opaque white page), Wayland.

## 2026-10-17
- Feature (lister): a click on the D/F/B line of the top bar cycles the byte totals (`%b`, `%B`) between bytes, megabytes and gigabytes. Sizes are written by `model::format_size_as` (the function the file sizes use), the plain byte count stays the default. New `status_format::ByteUnit` (`next`), kept in `StatusBar.byte_unit`; the click handler holds a `Weak<Ui>` (the `Ui` is owned by `StartState` for the whole run) and logs with `err_log!` when it is gone. The whole line is the click target, not only the B part. Tests: `the_unit_cycles_bytes_mega_giga`, `byte_totals_follow_the_unit`; clippy clean. NOT checked in a running Lister or in Xephyr (no GUI run here): check the click, then the usual Lister checklist. The unit is not saved, a new Lister starts in bytes.
- Fix (hub): the desktop menu no longer runs off the bottom of the screen. `popup_at` measured the menu while it was still hidden, and GTK gives a hidden widget a size of 0 (trace in Xephyr: `(0, 0)` hidden, `(163, 401)` once shown), so the clamp always used the fixed fallback `DESKTOP_MENU_H = 300` and a right-click near the bottom edge left About, Desktop and Quit below the screen. Now the menu is shown first and then measured and placed, in the same main-loop turn (no flicker); the clamp is the pure `menu_origin`. The icon menu goes through the same function. Tests: `a_menu_near_the_bottom_edge_is_moved_up_by_its_real_height`, `a_menu_with_room_opens_at_the_click`, `a_menu_taller_than_the_desktop_starts_at_the_top`. Checked in Xephyr (1280x800): right-click at the bottom right and bottom left shows the whole menu, Quit included; the Desktop submenu opened near the bottom keeps it on screen; a click near the top opens at the click; the icon menu of a bottom-row icon shows down to Delete; a click outside closes the menus. Smoke checks and DEST + F5 Copy passed.
- Fix (lister): typing fast in the path bar no longer leaves a suggested folder name in the path. Root cause, found with traces in Xephyr: the look-ahead (`wire_path_completion`) shows the suggestion as selected text, and GTK drops the selection of an entry when it believes it lost the X11 PRIMARY selection; during fast typing a late ownership notice (the primary clipboard turns `local=false` right after the suggestion was selected) made it deselect, so the next keys were inserted before the suggestion (`.../sub2` typed, `.../sub29e-e548f984aef4/` in the bar, Enter refused it and the bar went back). Now the key handler selects the suggestion again before GTK handles a key when it is still the end of the text, the cursor sits right before it and nothing is selected (`pathcomplete::ghost_to_restore`), and any change not made by the look-ahead forgets the suggestion, so a stale one is never selected again. Tests: `a_ghost_that_lost_its_selection_is_selected_again`, `a_ghost_is_left_alone_when_it_is_still_selected_or_gone`, `a_cursor_the_user_moved_is_not_overridden`, `positions_count_characters_not_bytes`. Checked in Xephyr typing a 100-character path at about 20 keys a second: before, 2 wrong paths in 12 tries; after, 30 right in 30, with the lost selection restored 36 times (`dlog` "the suggestion lost its selection"). Enter still accepts the suggestion, Backspace removes it, TAB accepts it and editing after TAB works. Smoke checks (path bar, double click, Backspace, Alt+Left/Right, global hotkeys, Alt+1/2, DEST + F5 Copy) passed. Not checked: Wayland (no PRIMARY race there, the code path is the same).
- Feature (lister): closing a Lister while a Function it started waits for a program asks what to do: "\"xterm\", started by a function of this Lister, is still open." with Leave Open (primary, Return: the program keeps running, the rest of the Function is skipped), Stop Program (`ToHub::StopFunctions`, the hub kills it) and Cancel (the window stays and the Function goes on). New `close_guard.rs`: `CloseGuard` (run id -> program from `ToLister::FunctionWaiting` / `FunctionWaitEnded`; `on_close_request` gives `Proceed`, `Ask(programs)` or `Wait` while the question is on screen, so a second click on X does not stack a second one) owned by `Ui.close_guard` for the whole run; `ask` / `on_answer` hold only a `Weak<Ui>` and log when it is gone. The requester always answers once, its window close counts as Cancel. Never asked: nothing waiting, a hub `Shutdown` and a lost hub (`allow_close` before `window.close()` in `inbound.rs` and the `HubGone` arm of `build.rs`). The close handler now logs a failed `Weak::upgrade` instead of returning silently. Tests: `nothing_waiting_closes_at_once`, `a_waiting_program_is_asked_about_once_until_answered`, `a_close_not_from_the_user_never_asks`, `the_question_names_the_programs`. Checked in Xephyr (scratch copy of the config, Function `Run xterm wait` + `Run touch AFTER-WAIT` on Ctrl+e, Listers opened on the scratch folder): no Function -> closes at once; program waited -> the question; a second X click -> no second question; Cancel -> the Lister stays and, once the xterm exits, the second step runs; the program ended -> closes without asking; Leave Open -> the Lister closes, the xterm stays, no second step after it; Stop Program -> the xterm is killed (hub log `asked to stop its function runs`), no second step; desktop menu Quit with a program waited -> the Lister closes without the question; the hub killed with SIGTERM -> the Lister closes without the question. Smoke checks (path bar, double click, Backspace, Alt+Left/Right, the four global hotkeys, Alt+1/2, DEST + F5 Copy) passed. Not checked: an old Lister (before protocol 3) against this hub in the running session.
- Change (hub, ipc, fsops): closing the Lister that started a Function no longer kills the program a `Run ... wait` (or External, or a script `exec`) step is waiting on. Since `9bc12cd` the drop of the requester cancelled the run and `wait_or_kill` killed the waited child: an editor started with wait died with unsaved work. Owner's choice: the default is now "leave it open"; the run still stops, the steps after the program do not run. New `CancelToken::cancel_sparing_programs` / `spares_programs` (fsops; a later `cancel()` wins, a sparing cancel never softens a full one); `FunctionRuns::forget_connection` uses it, `wait_or_kill` keeps waiting and logs `pid N is left running`. New `ToHub::StopFunctions` (`FunctionRuns::stop_runs_of`): cancel the runs of this connection and kill their programs, for the Lister's coming close dialog. New `FnEvent::Waiting` / `WaitEnded` around every waited program (`functions::wait_announced`: External, Run wait, Run window+wait, script `exec`), relayed as `ToLister::FunctionWaiting { run_id, program }` / `FunctionWaitEnded { run_id }` only to Listers that registered with `FUNCTION_WAIT_PROTOCOL` (3) or later (`Conn.version`, `speaks`), since an older Lister closes on an unknown message. `PROTOCOL_VERSION` 2 -> 3. The Lister only traces the two messages for now. Also drops the unused `recv` import left in `server.rs` by the previous commit. Tests: `a_sparing_cancel_cancels_and_spares_programs`, `a_full_cancel_wins_over_a_sparing_one_in_either_order`, `a_dropped_connection_spares_the_programs_its_runs_wait_on`, `stop_runs_of_kills_only_that_connection_and_wins_over_the_drop`, `a_waited_program_is_announced_before_and_after_the_wait`, `a_sparing_cancel_leaves_the_waited_program_running_and_skips_the_next_steps`, `a_sparing_cancel_leaves_a_run_window_program_running`, `a_waited_program_is_announced_only_to_listers_that_know_the_message`, `stop_functions_kills_the_waited_program`, `a_lister_that_goes_away_leaves_the_waited_program_running_and_the_run_stops`; the old kill tests (`cancelling_stops_*`) still pass. Checked in Xephyr on a scratch copy of the config with a Function `Run xterm wait` + `Run touch AFTER-WAIT` on Ctrl+e: the Lister gets `FunctionWaiting`; closing it leaves the xterm open (hub log line), closing the xterm then does not run the second step; without closing the Lister the second step runs and `FunctionDone` arrives. Smoke checks (path bar, double click, Backspace, Alt+Left/Right, the four global hotkeys, Alt+1/2, DEST + F5 Copy) passed. `StopFunctions` is exercised by the server test only until the Lister sends it.
- Fix (ipc, lister, hub): a message of an enum variant the reader does not know no longer ends the connection. New `frame::recv_tolerant` / `frame::Received`: a frame that is valid JSON for an externally tagged variant this build lacks comes back as `Received::Unknown(variant)`; a known variant with bad fields or a frame that is not JSON is still an error. The Lister reader thread (`client::start_reader`) and the hub `reader_loop` log `ignoring ... message <variant>` with `err_log!` and keep reading; before, an older Lister closed its window and the hub dropped the connection. This only helps builds from now on: a Lister older than this commit still closes on an unknown message. First step of the "confirm when a Lister with a waiting Function step is closed" work. Tests: `tolerant_recv_skips_unknown_variants_and_keeps_reading`, `tolerant_recv_still_fails_on_a_broken_known_message`, `reader_skips_an_unknown_hub_message_and_keeps_the_window`, `an_unknown_message_is_skipped_and_the_connection_stays` (replaces `an_unknown_message_is_a_decode_error_and_ends_the_connection`), `a_broken_message_is_a_decode_error_and_ends_the_connection`. Checked in Xephyr: path bar Enter, folder double click, Backspace, Alt+Left/Right, Ctrl+backslash, Ctrl+Shift+space, Ctrl+Print, Alt+1/2, a second Lister as DEST and an F5 Copy. The unknown-message path itself is only covered by the tests (no newer peer exists to send one).
- Audit (lister): who owns the `Weak` targets of `find_dialog.rs`, `scripts_ui.rs` and `script_editor.rs` (TODO item). No code changed; results and three follow-ups in `TODO.md` (Script Editor without an explicit owner once the Scripts window is closed, silent `Weak::upgrade()` failures, plus two older icon-view issues and a flaky polkit test found while checking the other fixes in Xephyr).
- Fix (viewer): a Markdown picture that gdk-pixbuf cannot decode is tried with GTK's own loaders (`gdk::Texture::from_filename` / `from_bytes`, on the same worker, at full size as before `91135e0`) before it falls back to `[image: alt]`. `Decoded` (pixbuf `RawImage` or `gdk::Texture`) crosses the thread; the size comes from the texture. Test: `gtk_decodes_a_picture_from_the_book_or_a_file_and_rejects_garbage`. Checked in Xephyr: a PNG in a `.md` still shows, an avif shows `[image: avif alt]` with the log line. NOT verified: a format GTK reads and gdk-pixbuf does not; on this machine (GTK 4.20.1, no glycin) GTK falls back to gdk-pixbuf, so avif / heif fail in both and the new branch only runs in the unit test.
- Fix (iconview): a file edited while its thumbnail is being made no longer keeps the old picture. Two holes: `ThumbTracker::begin` refused a new request while one was pending for the path, and the old result was applied by path; and `cache::store` stamped `Thumb::MTime` with the mtime read after the decode, so the old picture was cached under the new mtime and came back on every visit. Now `ThumbRequest` / `ThumbResult` carry the entry's `modified`, the tracker keys pending requests by (path, mtime) (`begin(path, modified)`, `finish(path, modified, ok)` returns false for a stale result, whose failure is not remembered), `apply_thumb_result` drops a result whose mtime no longer matches the entry, and the worker stamps the mtime read before decoding (`cache::store_made_at`, `cache::source_mtime`). Tests: `a_file_edited_while_pending_is_asked_again_and_the_old_result_is_stale`, `a_stale_failure_is_not_remembered`, `a_thumbnail_made_before_an_edit_is_not_valid_after_it`, `modified` round trip in `pool_serves_a_cached_image_without_a_generator`. Checked in Xephyr with a 6000x6000 noise PNG (about 1 s to decode) replaced by a blue one with a later mtime 0.5 s after it appeared: the master build kept the red thumbnail, this build shows the blue one.
- Fix (lister, iconview): the Reload command (Ctrl+R, palette, toolbar) asks again for the thumbnails that failed. Since `4c24cc5` a failure was retried only on an mtime or size-bucket change or after leaving the folder, so a missing or busy `ffmpeg` / `pdftoppm` or an unreadable file kept the generic icon. New `ThumbTracker::forget_failed`, `IconField::retry_failed_thumbs` and `Ui::reload_by_user` (palette `CommandId::Reload`); automatic reloads (watcher, jobs, hub) still call `Ui::reload` and keep skipping failures, so a broken file is not decoded on every change. Test: `forget_failed_lets_every_failure_retry_but_keeps_pending`, plus `retry_failed_thumbs` in the GTK scenario `field_thumbnail_wiring`. Checked in Xephyr: a `chmod 000` PNG shows the generic icon, `chmod 644` (watcher refresh, same mtime) keeps it, Ctrl+R shows the thumbnail.
- Fix (network-config): only a failed save cancels "Save then close". `on_done` cleared `close_after_save` on any `Done::Failed`, so a List or Load failing before the save ended left the window open after a good save. New `worker::SAVING` and `Done::is_failed_save`; `CloseState::failed` is called only for it. Test: `only_a_failed_save_is_a_failed_save`. NOT checked in the running app: making NetworkManager fail a List while a save runs is not practical; covered by the unit tests only.
- Fix (run): the Run window keeps the row the user picked when the PATH scan ends. `load_in_background` called `refresh()`, which selected row 0 again, also after the user moved with the arrow keys. Now the catalog index of the selected entry is read before the refresh and its row is selected again when it is still listed (`selected_entry`, `select_entry`, `row_of_entry`); the catalog only grows at its end, so the index stays valid. Typing still selects the first row. Tests: `the_picked_entry_keeps_its_row_after_the_scan`, `a_picked_entry_no_longer_listed_falls_back_to_the_first_row`. Checked in Xephyr with a PATH dir of 400000 files (scan of several seconds): typed `te`, Down Down to TeXInfo, the Commands rows appeared and TeXInfo stayed selected.
- Feature (lister, archive): opening a slow archive (a big `.tar.bz2`) shows a progress panel inside the Lister, centered over the window: a message with the entries read so far, a real progress bar (bytes read from the archive file by libarchive, `archive_filter_bytes(-1)`, against its size) and a Cancel button. It is an overlay child of the window (`gtk::Overlay` around the window content), so it has no frame, cannot be moved and follows the window. It appears only if the read still runs after 400 ms (cached archives and folders inside an archive never show it). While shown, the window content is insensitive and the key handlers swallow every key (Escape = Cancel, F4 terminal blocked); hub messages (`QuerySelection`, `ChangeDir`, ...) are still handled, and a navigation started by the hub hides the panel and supersedes the read. Cancel stops the libarchive read (the worker polls the token after every entry), stays in the folder on screen, restores the history and flashes "opening X cancelled"; a cancelled read is not cached. New: `opusdm_archive::list_watched` (+ `Reader::bytes_read`), `archive::ReadProgress` / `read_archive_rows_watched`, `progress_panel.rs` (`ProgressPanel`, owned by `Ui` for the whole run; the Cancel button holds only the callback slot, the callback holds a `Weak<Ui>` and logs when it is gone), `Ui::watch_archive_read` / `cancel_archive_read` in `nav_cache.rs`, CSS class `.opus-progress`. Tests: `a_watched_listing_reports_entries_and_bytes_read`, `a_watched_listing_stops_when_asked` (every format), `a_watched_read_reports_progress_and_a_cancelled_one_is_not_cached`, `the_progress_message_counts_the_entries_read`; no GTK test for the panel (a second `gtk::init` test in the binary would panic), `dlog!` traces "progress panel shown / hidden" instead. Checked in Xephyr with the release build and a 115 MB `.tar.bz2` (3000 entries, ~4.5 s): panel with count and moving bar, arrival in the archive, Escape cancel, Cancel button cancel (CPU back to 0%: the worker stops), Backspace / Parent click / F4 / Ctrl+L ignored while shown, folders inside the archive open without the panel; Lister checklist: Enter in the path bar, double click on a folder, Backspace, Parent, Alt+Left / Alt+Right, second Lister (star), Ctrl+backslash terminal, Alt+1 / Alt+2. NOT checked: Ctrl+Shift+space, Ctrl+Print, SOURCE/DEST roles, a Copy job, a function run, the real desktop session. Not covered by the panel: extracting a file from an archive to view it, archive Copy / drag-out extraction, remote listings. A watcher event in the folder on screen still supersedes the archive read (see TODO "Reload must not cancel a navigation"): the panel then closes and the Lister stays where it was.
- Feature (viewer): EPUB books are shown as one reflowed text in the Markdown view: title and author, then the chapters of the spine in order, with headings, paragraphs, bold / italic, lists, quotes, line breaks, preformatted text, web links and the pictures of the book (decoded from memory, scaled like Markdown pictures). Search (Ctrl+F, F3), the dark switch and the font size work as for Markdown. New `MediaKind::Ebook`, new `epub.rs` (container -> package document -> spine; each XHTML chapter turned into Markdown with every ASCII punctuation escaped, read with `quick-xml` 0.41 + `escape-html` for `&nbsp;` and friends); the book is read on a worker (`gio::spawn_blocking`) and shown only if it is still the file on screen. `MarkdownView::show_book` and an in-memory picture map (`loader::decode_within`); a Markdown file clears that map. Links inside the book (to other chapters, notes) are plain text; no CSS, no table layout, no cover thumbnail in the strip (placeholder `EPUB`). New default file type "Ebook file" (`*.epub`, double click = `opusdm-viewer`), seeded only on a new installation. Tests: `epub.rs` (spine order, title, escapes, whitespace, emphasis, blocks, links, hrefs, the parsed runs of the Markdown view), `decode_within`, keys, kinds. Checked in Xephyr with three real books (a 2600 magazine issue, two novels): cover pictures, text with spaces, italic, bold, links, Ctrl+F search with match count, next / previous book. NOT checked: the Lister double click (the owner's `~/.config/opusdm/filetypes.json` has no Comic / Ebook types: add them by hand), EPUB 3 books with SVG covers only, very large books.
- Feature (viewer): comic books (`.cbz` zip, `.cbr` rar) are shown like a PDF, one page at a time: page bar (Pages, previous / next), page panel with thumbnails (F9), Page Up / Down, Home / End, arrows, wheel past the edge, zoom and rotate; no search and no links. New `MediaKind::Comic` and `MediaKind::is_paged`; new `comic.rs` (pages = the pictures of the archive in natural name order, `__MACOSX/` and hidden files skipped, decoded from memory with the EXIF orientation); the PDF page worker and the page panel switch to `comic.rs` for a comic; the strip thumbnail extracts the first page only. New `opusdm_archive::read_files` (one pass over the archive, so a solid rar is decompressed once; the comic stays in memory compressed while it is on screen). New default file type "Comic file" (`*.cbz;*.cbr`, double click = `opusdm-viewer`); it is only seeded on a new installation, an existing `filetypes.json` must get it by hand. Tests: `comic.rs`, worker comic -> PDF -> comic, page panel thumbnails, keys, kinds, `read_files` on every archive format. Checked in Xephyr with the real viewer: an 85 MB `.cbr` (RAR v2, 100 pages) and a `.cbz` stored out of order: first page, Page Down, Home, End, F9 panel with thumbnails, strip thumbnails, next file to a PDF (search field back) and back to a comic (search field hidden). NOT checked: the Lister double click (needs the file type in the real `filetypes.json`), RAR5 archives, a comic in the Sidecar. webm was left for later at the owner's request (it is already a viewer video extension).
- Fix (lister): the scroll position is kept when rows are added or removed in the folder on screen (after a Delete, a watcher event, a reload). `apply_navigation` reads the scroll offset before `replace_changed_rows` and `Ui::keep_scroll` sets it back for 6 ticks of 20 ms (the adjustment clamps it when the list got shorter); it stops when another navigation starts, and logs if the window is gone. The cause (the list jumping on a splice) was inferred from the code, NOT reproduced: check in a running session by scrolling a long folder, deleting a file in the middle and at the end, in list and icon view. A wheel scroll within ~120 ms of a refresh is overridden.
- Feature (lister): Back, Forward, the history menu and Parent bring a folder back as it was left: scroll offset, keyboard cursor and selection (also when the folder was left by entering a subfolder or an archive). New `viewpos.rs` (`SavedViews`, at most 200 folders) filled by `Ui::save_view` when `navigate_from` leaves a folder; `Ui::restore_view` runs after the cached listing is shown, or after the disk read when nothing was cached (archives); the scroll is applied once the list is tall enough (25 tries, 20 ms). Plain navigation (path bar, double click, Home) still opens a folder at the top. New `Ui::navigate_restoring` is used by `go_parent`. Tests: `viewpos` (store only). NOT verified in a running session: scroll restore needs the real GTK layout; check long folder -> enter folder / open archive -> Back and Parent, in list and icon view, plus the usual Lister checklist.
- Fix (lister, style): the "Menu" and History buttons (`MenuButton`) looked flat next to the other toolbar buttons. The bevel rules matched `button.opus-btn` only, but a `MenuButton` carries the class on the `menubutton` wrapper and draws an inner `button`; the rules now also match `menubutton.opus-btn > button` (normal, hover, active/checked). New test `menu_buttons_get_the_same_bevel_as_the_toolbar_buttons`. NOT seen on screen. No `DropDown` exists in the Lister toolbar or footer, so the "select in the footer" part was not found and is untouched.
- Feature (lister, iconview): drag files / folders out of a Lister and drop them on a folder of the same Lister (icon view and details list). Default is Move, Ctrl held at drop is Copy; the folder under the pointer is highlighted (`drop-target` class: `.icon-item.drop-target`, `.row-dir/.row-file.drop-target`); a drop on a file or on the background is ignored, and so is a folder dropped on itself. Drops from other windows keep the old behaviour (Copy into the current folder, Shift = Move). New: `IconField::connect_drop_on_folder`, `dnd::ctrl_held`, `dnd::drop_on_folder`; a drag source in the field / column view records its paths (`file_drag`, `LIST_DRAG`) so only a drag of the same view can target its folders. Dropping into a folder shown inside an archive flashes "cannot drop into an archive". Tests: `field_folder_drop_target`, `a_folder_is_never_dropped_into_itself_or_below_itself`; `cargo test -p opusdm-lister -p opusdm-iconview` and clippy pass. NOT verified in a running session: no input injector here (xdotool), so the real drag, the highlight, Ctrl = Copy, and the Lister / hotkey / hub checklist of CLAUDE.md still have to be done by hand after `scripts/live-update.sh --all`.
- Fix (hub): a write error on a Lister socket (for example a frame over 16 MB) ended `writer_loop` silently while the reader kept the connection open, so that Lister never received `QuerySelection` again and hotkey functions waited forever. `writer_loop` now logs an `InvalidData` error, shuts the socket down so the hub drops the connection, and Run output batches are also capped at 256 KiB (`OUTPUT_BATCH_BYTES`). New tests: `a_write_that_fails_closes_the_connection_instead_of_leaving_it_deaf`, `batch_lines_caps_a_batch_at_max_bytes`. NOT checked in the desktop session.
- Fix (logging): the Lister hub-inbox loop and folder-watch loop log when the window is gone, a read error from the hub is logged before the window closes, and theme-config logs when the editor is closed before a Save / Save as copy / Duplicate finishes. No behaviour change. NOT checked in the desktop session.
- Docs: the open points of the audit of the 2026-10-16/17 optimizations are in `TODO.md`.
- Fix (lister): Enter in the path bar, double click on a folder and the global hotkeys did nothing. Since the `Weak<Ui>` change the only strong `Rc<Ui>` was a local of `connect_activate`, so it was dropped after the first listing; every handler then failed `upgrade()` and the hub inbox loop ended, so the hub's `QuerySelection` (needed before a hotkey function runs) was never answered. `StartState` now owns the `Ui` for the whole run. Checked: `cargo build --release -p opusdm-lister`, clippy. NOT checked in a desktop session until the Lister is restarted (`scripts/live-update.sh --all`).
- Fix (panel): clippy `absurd_extreme_comparisons` errors in `crates/panel/src/widgets/pager.rs`. The unsigned `rect.w` / `rect.h` checks `<= 0` are now `== 0` (same behaviour). `cargo clippy -p opusdm-panel --all-targets` has no errors left.
- Fixes from the second `OPTIMIZE.md` round (all 28 items ticked, one commit per item, five parallel branches plus one follow-up merged into master). Highlights:
  - **fsops / ftp / archive:** deleting or moving an SFTP symlink to a folder removes the link only (`RemoteItem::is_symlink`); Copy/Move recreate FIFOs, skip sockets and devices (never deleted by a Move) and `copy_file` refuses non-regular files; extraction refuses hardlinks to files it did not write (reproduced with a pax tar); a cross-disk Move deletes only what it copied; created folders get their source mode and times (deepest first); Delete drops the chmod retry and cancels inside a tree; Encrypt, Trash and Restore use `rename_noreplace`; Encrypt and Upload report skipped entries, an Upload-Move that skipped anything keeps its sources, a partial cross-disk Restore keeps its `.trashinfo`.
  - **hub / ipc:** cancelling a queued job reports a cancelled `Done` and a file-job step stops waiting on cancel; quit, watch teardown, icon/group saves and compositor reload no longer block the GTK thread (`io_worker.rs`); scripts run with Boa's `evaluate_async_with_budget` so a pure-JS loop can be cancelled; shutdown waits up to 3 s for queued saves; Lister-only messages need `Register` and a repeated one is ignored; `install_sigchld` errors are logged; output windows get ids from a table (`server/output_windows.rs`); config loaded once into `StartupConfig`; `RunJob` / `JobDone` carry an optional `tag` (`#[serde(default)]`, protocol stays 2).
  - **lister / iconview:** archive scratch folders are keyed by job tag and released only by their own jobs; `uris_to_paths` decodes bytes (no panic, non-UTF-8 kept, `localhost` stripped); thumbnail generator stdout read while it runs; `ThumbTracker` with pending/failed sets, failures reported by the pool; cache writes through `NamedTempFile`; texture cache trimmed above 600; `parse_archive_path` skips `#` in folder names; one `dnd::file_uri`; `sort_by_cached_key`; failed inotify watches logged with a status hint on the watch limit.
  - **panel / shell:** CSS installed once; new `opusdm_ui::watch_layout` coalesces monitor changes and skips unchanged rects (panel, dock); EWMH re-reads only moved windows with pipelined requests; Wayfire requests time out after 2 s, the watcher keeps one connection and panel clicks go to a worker; a hovered notification keeps its timer stopped; `PANEL_HEIGHT` lives in `opusdm_ipc`; dock colours are named constants; the Run window scans PATH off the GTK thread with lowercase keys cached in a `Catalog`.
  - **apps:** polkit gives up after 3 helper failures without a prompt; shot asks before replacing a file behind an added extension and saves off the GTK thread; viewer applies EXIF orientation and loads Markdown pictures on a worker (`loader::load_within`); backlights ranked by `type`; volume changes keep channel balance (slider shows the loudest channel); apps-editor writes atomically and refuses to write when the source cannot be read.
  - Tests: `cargo test --workspace` passes (2333 tests, 17 ignored, 80 suites). NOT checked in a desktop session: every GUI behaviour above, Wayfire with a live compositor (one connection for several requests is assumed), real PulseAudio, a real multi-monitor mode switch. Leftovers are listed in `OPTIMIZE.md`. Pre-existing clippy errors in `crates/panel/src/widgets/pager.rs` are untouched.
- Docs: new `OPTIMIZE.md` for the second review round (28 items). No code changed.

## 2026-10-17
- Tooling: new `scripts/clear.sh` frees disk space on demand. It deletes the `target/` of every agent worktree in `.claude/worktrees/` and the incremental caches of the main `target/`; `--all` also runs `cargo clean` on the workspace, `--dry-run` only prints what would go. It refuses to run while `cargo` or `rustc` is running and never touches sources or branches. Checked: `sh -n`, `--dry-run`, `--dry-run --all`, unknown option (exit 2). NOT run for real.

## 2026-10-16
- Fixes from `OPTIMIZE.md` (all 27 items ticked, one commit per item, five parallel branches merged into master plus one follow-up pass). Highlights:
  - **fsops / ftp / archive:** a cross-filesystem Move no longer deletes the sources the user skipped; copied symlinks ask before replacing an entry and are renamed into place; files and folders are fsynced before a Move deletes the source; FTP/SFTP downloads go to a temp file (new `RemoteError::Cancelled`) and copy in 256 KiB chunks that cancel mid-file; compress drains the tool's stderr while it runs; extract removes a partly written entry.
  - **hub / ipc:** the accept loop survives transient errors; Run steps wait through `reaped` so the exit status is not lost; cancel kills running programs and ends confirm/alert waits; file types and button banks are cached by stamp (`ipc::stamp_cache`); `send_or_log`; per-client queues bounded at 4096, broadcasts encoded once, one fit-check worker; `resolve_steps`, split `FnEvent` handlers, op table for scripts, `function::keys` constants; GIO file URIs; `sockpath::send_oneshot`; `PROTOCOL_VERSION` (2) in `Register`/`Welcome`; Run output batched as `ToLister::OutputLines` (every 50 ms or 256 lines).
  - **lister:** user hotkeys win over Alt+Left/Right; GIO file URIs; cached file types and bookmarks (`config_cache.rs`), path completion on a worker; `Ui::send_hub`; batch rename on a worker with the full error list; `background.rs` helpers with cancel tokens; shared `Rc<[Rc<Row>]>` listings with interned owner names; `Weak<Ui>` handlers and `Ui::shutdown`; `apply_inbound` split into `inbound.rs`, navigation state in one `Navigation` struct.
  - **panel / iconview / shared:** sysmon timer stops with its bar and scans processes on a worker; screen height read live; per-window EWMH cache and in-place tasklist updates; `IconField`/`IconItem` handlers hold weak references and listeners are copied before calling; new `opusdm_watch::DbusError`, new crate `opusdm-apps` (re-exported by the dock), shared X11 window setup helpers in `opusdm-x11` (used by panel, dock, notify, hub desktop); NetworkManager without property caching and with a `StateChanged` wait; ddcutil bus cached; hub group windows hold the field weakly.
  - **config apps:** network-config waits for the save before closing; shot editor and theme icon picker no longer panic; terminal, shot, theme Apply and apps-editor report save failures; input-config, theme save/import/export and the screen tool run off the GTK thread; viewer, player, network-config and the Theme Editor release their state on close; new `opusdm-ui` helpers (`section_heading`, `labeled_row`, `clear_children`, `pick_file`, `DirtyGuard`, `confirm_discard`).
  - Tests: `cargo test --workspace` passes (2251 tests in 80 suites). NOT checked in a desktop session: every GUI behaviour above, the X11 and Wayland window setup, a real power loss, a real NetworkManager connect. Left over: `External` step output is still sent line by line, FTP has no per-byte progress.
- Docs: new `OPTIMIZE.md` at the root, a prioritized checklist of 27 optimization and quality items found by a code review of the whole workspace (1 Critical, 11 High, 10 Medium, 5 Low). No code changed.
- Fix (ui, all apps, build-release): the `opusdm-*` apps showed the generic application icon. `opusdm-ui` gets `set_app_icon()` (GTK default window icon name `opusdm`), called from `connect_startup` of every app (hub, lister, settings, dock, run, shot, terminal, player, viewer, input-config, screen, apps-editor, network-manager, polkit). `build-release.sh` stages `assets/ball.png` as `hicolor/512x512/apps/opusdm.png` and one hidden `org.opusdm.<Id>.desktop` per app (Wayland compositors map the app id to the icon through it); `install.sh` installs both and refreshes the icon cache. The Openbox theme copy now uses `openbox-share/.` so `dist/share` can hold more than the theme. Compiles; NOT checked in a session: needs `install.sh` to be run, and the Lister tool windows started by the hub with other app ids have no desktop entry.

## 2026-10-15
- Feature (hub, ipc, lister): scripts get **`opus.alert(message)`** (modal dialog with one OK button, new `ToLister::AlertPrompt` answered with `ConfirmReply`; with no Lister to show it the message is logged and the script goes on) and **`opus.notify(title, {body, icon, timeout, urgency})`** (a desktop notification sent by the hub to `org.freedesktop.Notifications`, returns its id).
- Feature (viewer): on a PDF the **mouse wheel** scrolls the page instead of zooming; pushed on past the bottom edge it turns to the next page shown from its top, past the top edge to the previous page shown from its bottom, so the pages read like one long picture (a page that fits the window turns on every notch; touchpad pixel deltas add up to one notch before a turn). **Ctrl+wheel** zooms around the pointer (pictures keep the plain wheel zoom, Ctrl+wheel works there too). New pure `page_wheel.rs` (`wheel_move`, `notch_pixels`, GTK's step `page^(2/3)`); `ImageView` gets `set_page_scroll`, `connect_page_edge` and `replace_image(raw, Landing::{Keep, Top, Bottom})`; the window turns the page in `_pdf_edge` and remembers the landing in `PdfUi::land_bottom`. Two buttons **<** / **>** (`go-previous-symbolic` / `go-next-symbolic`) next to **Pages** in the PDF bar go to the previous / next page (like Page Up / Page Down), insensitive on the first / last page. Tests: 5 in `page_wheel.rs` (123 pass in the crate). Checked under Xephyr with an XTest wheel on a 5 page A4 test PDF: Ctrl+wheel from `Fit (39%)` to `76%`, the wheel scrolls page 1 down to its bottom and on through the next pages, back up past the top of page 4 shows page 3 from its bottom, `>` / `<` show page 4 / 3 from the top. NOT checked: a real touchpad, Wayland.
- Feature (hub, ipc, lister, ui, viewer): **JavaScript scripts** with the Boa engine (`boa_engine` 0.22, pure Rust, default features off). Spec in `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-15-javascript-scripting-design.md`, user guide section 3.39. Scripts are `~/.config/opusdm/scripts/<name>.js` (new `ipc/src/scripts.rs`: list, load, atomic save, rename, delete, name check, description from the leading `//` lines, template, the API reference table). New internal command **Run Script** (`InternalCmd::Script`, parameters `script` with the new `ParamKind::Script` drop-down, and `args` with placeholders) and reference prefix `script:<name>` (`FuncRef::Script`, new **Script** kind in the shared reference picker), so a script runs from Functions, hotkeys, user menu, Script Events, button banks, file types, the palette and the Command Line. Hub: the per-step `match` of the executor is now `functions::run_step`, which scripts reuse; new `hub/src/scripting.rs` runs a script on the executor thread with one native `__opusHost(op, payload)` (JSON in and out, failures thrown as `Error`) under the `opus` object of `scripting/prelude.js`: `source` / `dest` / `listers` (`go`, `files`, `refresh`), `command` (with `files` / `to`), `commands`, `runFunction`, `runScript`, `run`, `exec`, `shell`, `log` / `console`, `confirm`, `sleep`, `fs`, `path`, desktop and bank shortcuts. What only the hub knows goes through the new `FnEvent::ScriptQuery` (Listers with role and path, saved Functions, `GoTo` a Lister, `Refresh`). Nesting limit 8, cancel checked at every call, `async` code settles before the step ends. A run made only of Script steps may start without a SOURCE Lister (in `$HOME`). Lister: new **Scripts** window (`opusdm-lister --scripts`, palette **Scripts (JavaScript)...**, Settings Advanced tab): filter, list, New / Edit / Run / Rename / Duplicate / Delete / Show Folder, output pane, folder watched for changes. New editor: syntect colouring (recoloured after typing pauses), line number gutter, Ln / Col, `*` when unsaved, Ctrl+S, F5 / Ctrl+Enter run the unsaved text with an Arguments field, Tab / Shift+Tab, auto indent, Ctrl+/ comments, ask before closing with changes; errors in red with their line marked (Boa gives a stale position for "x is not defined", the editor finds the first use of `x`); reference panel with the API and every internal command, double click inserts. The highlighter moved from the viewer to the new crate `opusdm-highlight` (with the dark / light palettes) and the line number gutter to `opusdm-ui`, both shared by viewer and editor. Tests: 4 in `ipc/scripts.rs`, 1 in `funcref.rs`, 17 in `hub/scripting/tests.rs`, 2 hub server tests (script from a tool window without Listers, Listers query), 5 in `lister/script_editor.rs`, 1 in `reference_picker.rs`. Checked with screenshots on Xvfb with the real hub: list, editor colours / gutter / indentation / comment toggle, a run listing the SOURCE Lister's files, a failing run marking the right line. NOT checked: Wayland, the light theme, a script started from a hotkey or a button bank.

## 2026-10-14
- Feature (panel): **calendar and appointments on the clock**. A left click on the panel clock (now a flat `MenuButton`) opens a `gtk::Calendar` popover on today; its arrows change month and year, and the days with appointments are marked (bold, underlined, refreshed on every month / year change). A click on a day number (capture phase `GestureClick`, picked widget with the `day-number` class, so the arrows do not count) closes the popover and opens the day window (new `agenda_window.rs`): the appointments of the day by time (a bell icon when they have an alarm) and a form with **Text**, **Date** (from the click, read only), **Time** (`HH:MM` or `H.MM`, checked) and **Alarm** (No alarm, 5, 10, 20, 30 mins, 1 hour). The first appointment of the day is loaded in the form; a click on a row loads that one; **Add** / **Save** store it, **Delete** removes it, **New** empties the form, **Close** or Escape closes the window; a second day click replaces the open window. Several appointments per day. New `agenda_model.rs` (pure): `Agenda` / `Appointment` in `~/.config/opusdm/appointments.json`, ids, `on_day`, `busy_days`, `take_due` (an alarm rings once from its moment; one missed while the panel was off still rings up to 60 min after the start, later it is dropped), editing re-arms an alarm only when its moment moved; times compared as local wall clock minutes. New `agenda_center.rs`, shared by all bars: the agenda in memory, the file, a 15 s alarm timer that sends `org.freedesktop.Notifications.Notify` (app `Calendar`, icon `x-office-calendar`, urgency critical so it pops in Do not disturb and stays until dismissed, body `09:30 (in 10 min)`). Tests: 9 in `agenda_model.rs`, 1 in `agenda_center.rs` (139 pass in the crate). Checked under Xephyr with openbox and the standalone panel with screenshots: popover, month arrow (no window), day 20 window, Add with 10 mins, file written, reopen loaded in the form, Delete; with `opusdm-notify` in the same bus an alarm already due popped as `Call Marco / 07:54 (in 3 min)` and was saved as `notified`. NOT checked: Wayland (layer shell popover and the day window), the hub itself.
- Fix (panel): after using the right click menu of a bell history entry (**Remove**) the panel stopped taking clicks (pager, bell, tray). Probable cause: that menu was a popover attached to the history row, and the refresh that followed the removal destroyed the row while the popover was still open, which can leave GTK's input grab on the panel window. The menu is gone: each history row now has two small buttons, **×** (Remove) and a mute button (**Mute <app>** / **Unmute <app>** in its tooltip). Checked in the hub sandbox under Xephyr: after removing an entry with ×, a click on the pager switches desktop. The old failure was NOT reproduced on purpose to confirm the cause.
- Fix (notify): notifications from Brave could not be muted: the bell's right click menu had only **Remove**. Brave (Chromium) sends an empty app name and a `desktop-entry` of `org.chromium.Chromium`. A notification without a name now takes the name of the sending process (`/proc/PID/comm` of the sender's bus connection, first letter capitalized: `Brave`), else its `desktop-entry`; the popup, the history and the mute list use it (`Notification::fill_app_name`, `server::process_name`). 1 new test (34 in the crate). Checked in a private bus: a nameless `Notify` from `gdbus` is listed as `Gdbus`. NOT checked with Brave itself.
- Feature (notify, panel, hub, ipc): **desktop notifications**. Notifications from browsers (WhatsApp Web in Brave), mail clients and `notify-send` were silently lost in an OpusDM session: nothing owned `org.freedesktop.Notifications` (only Plasma's `plasma_waitforname` stub was activatable). New crate `crates/notify`, binary `opusdm-notify`, started by the hub next to the polkit agent (a second copy exits; an existing server such as dunst is never replaced, `RequestName` with `DoNotQueue` only). Spec in `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-14-notification-server-design.md`. Server (`server.rs`): `Notify` / `CloseNotification` / `GetCapabilities` (`actions`, `body`, `body-markup`, `body-hyperlinks`, `icon-static`, `persistence`) / `GetServerInformation`, signals `NotificationClosed`, `ActionInvoked`, `ActivationToken` (Wayland). Hints (`model.rs`): urgency, `image-data` (validated stride and length, RGB / RGBA 8 bit), `image-path`, `desktop-entry`, `transient`, `resident`. Store (`store.rs`): routing (critical always pops, DND or muted app goes to the history only, transient never to the history), `replaces_id` updates in place, ids never 0, history of 100 in memory, unread count, timeouts (default 6 s, low 3 s, critical never); a notification closes only when it is neither on screen nor in the history, so a click in the history still reaches the app. Body markup whitelist (`markup.rs`): `b`, `i`, `u`, `a href` kept and balanced, everything else escaped. Popups (`popup.rs`): 360 px windows stacked from a corner (`placement.rs`), at most 5 shown and the rest queued, hover pauses the timer, click runs the default action, right click / × dismisses, up to 3 action buttons, fade-in; Wayland overlay layer surface, X11 `_NET_WM_WINDOW_TYPE_NOTIFICATION` plus above / sticky / skip taskbar / skip pager asked after map, on the monitor under the pointer minus the struts. Settings in `~/.config/opusdm/notifications.json` (`corner`, `timeout_ms`, `dnd`, `mute`). Private interface `org.opusdm.Notifications1` (`control.rs`, names and tuples in `ipc/src/notify.rs`): `GetState`, `Activate`, `Remove`, `ClearHistory`, `MarkRead`, `SetDnd`, `Mute`, `Unmute`, signal `Changed`. Panel: a bell at the right end of the primary bar (`notify_client.rs` thread, `notify_center.rs`, `widgets/notifications.rs`) with a red unread badge (`9+`), struck while Do not disturb is on, dimmed when the server is not running; its drop-down has the DND switch, the history (click runs the action, right click Remove / Mute app) and Clear all; opening it marks all read; middle click flips DND. Release and live-update scripts list the new binary. Tests: 33 in `opusdm-notify` (one live D-Bus test, `#[ignore]`, run with `dbus-run-session -- cargo test -p opusdm-notify -- --include-ignored`), 2 in `ipc/notify.rs`, 4 in the panel. Checked with screenshots: in Xephyr with openbox (popups, markup and link, `image-data`, Reply button click sends `ActionInvoked(1, "reply")` then `NotificationClosed(1, 2)`, the hub sandbox with the bell, history, DND and the struck bell, popups kept out of the task list) and in headless labwc (layer surface stack). NOT checked: Brave / WhatsApp Web for real, multi-monitor placement, the Wayland activation token. Note: Ubuntu's AppArmor profile of `notify-send` only allows `GetServerInformation` and `Notify`, so `notify-send -A` reports no action support with any server.
- Fix (lister): the Trash window list did not use the Lister colours (light grey text on the grey ground, GTK's own selection). Its `ColumnView` now gets the `data-table` class and its cells the Lister classes `row-file` / `row-dir` and `cell-selected` (`columns::apply_selected_class`, on bind and on `selected` notify), so file / folder / selected colours come from the Lister theme stylesheet. NOT checked by eye.
- Fix / feature (lister, fsops): the Trash window selected badly (a `ListBox` in multiple mode). It is now a `ColumnView` over a `MultiSelection` like the Lister: click, Ctrl / Shift click, Ctrl+A, resizable columns, headers sort by Name, Original location or Deleted (newest first by default), tooltip with the full original path. New **Restore To...** button: asks for a folder (the Lister's current one proposed, `~` expanded) and restores the selected items there under their original names (`trash_items::restore_to`; the folder must exist; never overwrites). Restore on another disk now works too: on `EXDEV` the entry goes through the copy engine and is deleted afterwards (`move_across`, a conflict is skipped, never overwritten). `TrashItem::original_name` moved from the UI to fsops. Tests: 3 new in `trash_items.rs` (17 trash tests pass). NOT checked in the GUI.
- Feature (lister, fsops): **Show Trash** palette command (`CommandId::ShowTrash`, File Ops) opens a Trash window (new `lister/src/trash_ui.rs`, `DialogShell` 820x480): multi-select list with Name (original name, `/` after folders), Original location and Deleted columns, newest first, a count line, and **Restore**, **Delete** (also `Del`, with confirmation), **Empty Trash...** (with confirmation). The work runs on a thread with the window insensitive, then the list is read again; failures are shown in a requester with one line per item. New `fsops/src/trash_items.rs`: `trash_dirs` (home trash plus `.Trash/$uid` and `.Trash-$uid` at every mount top from `/proc/self/mounts`, octal escapes decoded, pseudo filesystems skipped), `list_items` / `list_all` (reads `info/*.trashinfo`, only the `[Trash Info]` group, percent-decodes `Path`, relative paths start at the top of the disk, info files without their entry are left out), `restore` (never overwrites, recreates a missing folder, removes the info), `erase`, `empty` (also entries without info and `directorysizes`). `delete::remove_recursive` is now `pub(crate)`. Tests: 11 in `trash_items.rs`, 2 in `trash_ui.rs`. NOT checked in the GUI (the Lister needs the running hub).
- Feature (lister, fsops, ipc): **Del** now moves the selection to the freedesktop.org trash and **Shift+Del** deletes it permanently, both after a requester (`Move N item(s) to the trash?` / `Delete N item(s) permanently?`). The Delete button of the row does the same: left click trash, right click permanent (`actions::wire_delete`, release-time gesture like Copy / Move; the duplicate `actions::delete_selection` is gone, the button now goes through `commands::delete_selection(ui, permanent)`, so it also honours the Sidecar). With a Sidecar open both keys act on the shown file only (`sidecar::delete_shown(ui, permanent)`). New `Op::Trash` and `fsops/src/trash.rs` (no new dependency): home trash `$XDG_DATA_HOME/Trash` when the file is on its device, else `$top/.Trash/$uid` (only when `.Trash` is a sticky, non-symlink folder) or `$top/.Trash-$uid` at the top of the mount; the `.trashinfo` is created exclusively first to reserve the name (`name`, `stem.2.ext`, ...), holds the percent-encoded `Path` (relative to the top for mount trashes) and the local `DeletionDate`, and is removed again when the move fails; folders created with mode 0700; a device without usable trash reports an error in the job (no silent permanent delete). New palette command `CommandId::DeletePermanent` ("Delete Selected Items Permanently", Shift+Del); `CommandId::Delete` is now "Move Selected Items to Trash". The row menu **Delete...**, the hub desktop and Function Delete steps stay permanent; remote folders refuse the trash (message now points to Shift+Del). Tests: 7 in `trash.rs`, Shift+Del in `keys.rs`. NOT checked in the GUI (needs the running hub).
- Fix (lister): after Del (with the Sidecar) the Down arrow jumped back to the top of the list. The Lister moved its own cursor and the selection, but the arrows follow GTK's keyboard focus row, which stayed on the deleted row and fell to the top once it was gone. `Ui::scroll_to_cursor` (so `jump_to_row`, Insert, type-to-find and Find too) now also gives the cursor row the focus (`focus_cursor_row`): GTK 4.10 has no call for it, so the Name column keeps weak refs of its list items (`columns::row_widget`) and the row widget of the cursor position grabs the focus, retried every 20 ms up to 10 times until the row is built; nothing happens while a text field has the keyboard. Checked under Xephyr with XTest keys on a folder of 10 pictures: Sidecar on f04, Del + Enter: f05 selected and shown, Down goes to f06; on the last row f10, Del + Enter: f09, Up goes to f08.
- Feature (lister, viewer): **Del** with a Sidecar open deletes only the file the Sidecar shows (confirm `Delete "name" permanently?`) and moves on to the next one; before, it deleted the whole selection and the Lister lost its place after the reload. The viewer now writes the file on screen back on its stdout (same NUL format, one path or none; `State::_report_shown` after `show_current` and `_show_nothing`) and the Lister reads it on a thread (`Sidecar::shown`). The reported file is used only when it is one of the paths last sent or inside one of them, else the only file sent (`sidecar::pick_shown`), so a late answer never deletes the wrong file; a Sidecar showing nothing leaves Del as before. Before the job the Lister steps past the file (`_step_past`): the next row (the previous at the end) for a single row, which the reload then keeps; just its own row out of a bigger selection; for a file inside a shown folder the paths are sent again after `JobDone` (`sidecar::job_done`). The viewer keeps its place across updates (`Gallery::keep_place`: same file if still there, same position if files were only removed, else the first). Tests: `keep_place` in `gallery.rs`, `pick_shown` in `lister/sidecar.rs`. Pipe smoke test: the viewer reports the shown file and keeps it when the selection shrinks or the folder is sent again. NOT checked from a real Lister (needs the hub).
- Feature (lister, viewer, ipc): **Sidecar** viewer. **Shift+F3** (palette **Toggle Sidecar Viewer**, `CommandId::ToggleSidecar`) starts `opusdm-viewer --sidecar` with a pipe on its stdin; on every selection change (`selection_changed` in `build.rs`) and after every navigation the Lister writes the selected paths, or the cursor row when nothing is selected, keeping only local paths (new `lister/src/sidecar.rs`, `Ui::sidecar`; an unchanged selection is not resent; a write error means the user closed the viewer and it is forgotten). The Lister presents its own window again 400 ms after the start so the arrows keep walking the list. Wire format in the new `ipc/src/sidecar.rs`: each path ends with NUL, an empty path ends the update. Viewer side (new `viewer/src/window/sidecar.rs`): a thread reads stdin, the main loop keeps only the newest update of a burst and replaces the gallery (`Gallery::empty`, `Strip::set_items`); folders and known kinds only, anything else shows *Nothing to show*; EOF (Shift+F3 again or the Lister closing, `sidecar::stop` in the close handler) closes the window; deleting the last file empties a Sidecar instead of closing it; `Escape` works on an empty Sidecar. The window keeps its size. Tests: 2 in `ipc/sidecar.rs`, 1 in `lister/sidecar.rs`, 1 in `viewer/window/sidecar.rs`, Shift+F3 in `keys.rs`, `args.rs` updated. Smoke test on the real X display driving the viewer through its pipe: 12 picture updates, a folder, an unknown file, an empty update and a burst of 80 updates, window stayed 1000x720, closing the pipe exits with 0. NOT checked by eye from a real Lister (needs the running hub). `docs/usage.md` 3.3 updated.
- Fix (lister): one very long file name (104 characters in `~/Downloads`) made the Name column as wide as that name, pushing Size, Date and the rest far right behind a horizontal scroll. The Name label is now ellipsized in the middle (`EllipsizeMode::Middle`, the extension stays visible), so the column fits the window and the other columns stay in view. Builds; NOT checked by eye in the GUI (the Lister needs the running hub).
- Feature (viewer): zooming a picture or a PDF page (wheel, **+** / **-**, bar buttons) keeps the point under the mouse pointer in place, or the middle of the view when the pointer is outside it; before, the scroll position stayed put and the picture grew from its top-left corner. `ImageView` tracks the pointer (`EventControllerMotion` on the scrolled window) and `_set_zoom_anchored` scrolls to the new position; it gives the adjustments the new size at once, so the first frame is already in place, and sets the value again after the layout. The math is the new pure `zoom::anchored_scroll` (content shorter than the view is centred, result kept in range); 3 new tests, 115 pass in the crate. Checked under Xephyr with an XTest driven wheel on a 1600x1200 test picture: a dot under the pointer stayed within 2 px over 5 zoom-in and 3 zoom-out notches; below fit the picture is centred, as before.
- Fix (viewer): the info bar zoom percentage now follows the mouse wheel too (it stayed at `Fit (58%)`): `ImageView::connect_wheel_zoom` runs a callback after every wheel zoom and the window updates the info bar with it. Checked under Xephyr: two wheel notches from `Fit (58%)` show `90%`.
- Change (lister): Quick Look is removed: the `👁` button, the **Quick Look (Preview)** row menu entry, the `PreviewWindow` and its live update on selection change. **F3** now opens the selection (files and folders) in `opusdm-viewer`, like the row menu **Open in Viewer** (new palette command `CommandId::OpenViewer`, "Open in Viewer", replaces `CommandId::Preview`; `context_menu::open_in_viewer` is now public; with nothing selected the status line says *nothing selected*). `Action::PreviewOrSize` is gone, so **Space** is no longer bound (it also computed folder sizes on a folder selection; that stays on the row menu). `preview.rs` is renamed `file_kind.rs` and keeps only `FileKind`, `kind_of`, `IMAGE_EXTS` and `generate_hex_dump` for the text Viewer (`detect_file_kind` and the window loaders went with their tests). 1 new test in `keys.rs` (F3 -> OpenViewer, Space unbound); 362 pass in the crate. `docs/usage.md` sections 2, 3.3, 3.10f, 3.28 and the shortcut table updated. NOT checked by eye in the GUI.
- Feature (viewer): two zoom buttons (`zoom-out-symbolic` / `zoom-in-symbolic`) in the bar, before the **Dark** switch. On a Markdown or source text they change the font size by 10% between 50% and 300% (new `font_scale.rs`: `FontScale` keeps one `CssProvider` on the display, priority application + 1, with `textview.viewer-doc-text { font-size: N%; }`; both text views get the `viewer-doc-text` class, so the Markdown heading scales and the line number gutter follow). The size is shared by the two views for the session, not remembered; the info bar shows `  -  120%` when it is not 100%. On a PDF the buttons call the page zoom (`ImageView::zoom_in` / `zoom_out`, like `+` / `-`). Window side: `State::font`, `_zoom_document` in `window/pdf.rs`. 3 unit tests in `font_scale.rs` (112 pass in the crate); the viewer starts on a source file with no GTK / CSS warning.
