The long-lived connections (tray, EWMH observer, screen watcher, global keys) are unchanged. `x11::tests::shared_connection_interns_once_and_moves_a_window` (ignored, needs `DISPLAY`) checks it against a live server.
- Refactor (hub, lister, buttonbank): the hub now owns the Functions, like the bindings. New `ToHub::SetFunctions`, `ToLister::FunctionsUpdate`, `Welcome.functions` and `HubEvent::FunctionsChanged`; the hub keeps `current_functions` (loaded once, written by the persist thread). Listers keep a copy in `HubConnection.functions` instead of reading `functions.json` on every right-click, palette open, hotkey key press and bank button check; the Function Editor saves on top of the hub's latest list (`upsert_function`, `delete_function`), so two Listers no longer overwrite each other's file. The hub's bank windows and bank editor read the same copy (`Hooks.functions`); `palette::get_commands` takes the function list.
- Fix (hub): Function steps Copy, Move, Delete, Copy As, Move As and Duplicate now run on the hub's `JobEngine` (new `FnEvent::Job`, `Hub::function_job`; `FnEvent::Conflict` and `function_conflict` are gone). They get the progress window and cancel button, the standard conflict prompt and the `InvalidatePath` refresh of the open Listers. Each run has a `CancelToken` (`function_cancels`): when its Lister closes, the run stops before its next step and its jobs are cancelled. A conflict nobody can answer is skipped (hub jobs included) and `conflict_owners` covers every prompt. A cancelled job fails its step. Shutdown hooks run such jobs in place with `functions::run_job_inline`.
- Perf (lister): `select_names` matches rows against a `HashSet` and applies them with one `set_selection` call, so restoring a large selection after a reload fires `selection-changed` (status line, preview) once instead of once per row.
- Fix (lister): auto-refresh no longer clears the filter being typed. `apply_navigation` resets the filter bar only when the folder changes, so `on_directory_changed` is a plain `reload()` (the save and restore of the filter text, which raced with the asynchronous listing, is gone).
- Fix (lister): the rename dialogs (button, command, context menu) build the target from the renamed file's own folder (`actions::renamed_sibling`, `rename_and_reload`) instead of the Lister's current folder when OK is pressed, and reject empty names, `.`, `..` and names containing `/`.
- Fix (rename): new `opusdm_fsops::rename_noreplace` (`renameat2(RENAME_NOREPLACE)`, with a check-then-rename fallback) replaces `std::fs::rename` at all 7 call sites (Lister rename dialog, command, context menu, icon view, batch rename, Function Rename step, desktop rename), so a rename never overwrites an existing file. Batch rename (`rename_all`) goes through temporary names, so chains such as `a -> b, b -> c` are order independent and a clashing entry is restored and reported.
- Fix (fsops): an overwriting `copy_file` writes a hidden temp file next to the destination and renames it over the target only after success. A cancel, a full disk or a read error now keeps the old file and removes the temp file.
- Fix (fsops, hub): `copy_file` compares `(dev, ino)` of source and destination before opening for overwrite and refuses with `InvalidInput`, so a DEST reached through a symlink or bind mount can no longer truncate the source. Function steps (Copy, Move, Delete, ...) now run `validate_op` like the job engine does.
- Fix (hub): connection ids only ever grow (`free_conns` is gone), so a stale `ListerEof` / `FromLister` from a dead reader thread can no longer hit a Lister that connected later.
- Docs: new `OPTIMIZE.md`, a prioritised list of 22 code review findings (connection id reuse in the hub, copy / rename data-loss risks, Lister refresh issues, duplicated X11 helpers, and so on).
- Fix (dock): the dock now sits on the configured edge. openbox ignored the position of the plain `ConfigureWindow` the dock sent, so the dock stayed where the `Smart` placement put it (top left, or the middle of the screen with `<center>yes</center>`); `window::pin_geometry` now also sends `_NET_MOVERESIZE_WINDOW`, like the hub desktop window. The X connections used for the dock hints now wait for a round trip (`window::sync`) before closing: closing right after a flush lost the `_NET_WM_STATE` messages, so the dock was never BELOW, SKIP_TASKBAR or SKIP_PAGER. The states are re-sent with the delayed pins after map, once openbox has adopted the window. The dock size counts the stylesheet border (`placement::BORDER`), so the geometry matches the size GTK insists on.
- Fix (panel): the taskbar leaves out windows with `_NET_WM_STATE_SKIP_TASKBAR` (the dock, the button banks).
- Fix (X11): new `opusdm_ui::x11_sync` (a round trip before a short-lived `x11rb` connection is dropped), used by every function that opens a connection, sends window manager requests and closes it: the dock (`window.rs`, replaces its local `sync`), the hub desktop window (`desktop.rs`), the panel (`window.rs`: geometry, hints, raise above; `ewmh.rs`: click actions) and the button banks (`x11.rs`: skip taskbar, move). Long-lived connections (tray, EWMH observer, screen watcher) are unchanged.
- Feature: the theme is the single source of the look. Colours, appearance (type indicators, monospace list, themed window), fonts and the Lister / requester pictures are edited only in the Theme Editor; the Settings window keeps only preferences (active theme, light / dark, wallpaper, icon grid, desktop folder, panel, dock, menu, behaviour). Spec and plan: `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-02-theme-single-source-design.md`, `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-10-02-theme-single-source.md`.
- Applying a theme is no longer a partial merge: `apply::resolve_look` (replaces `merge_custom_theme`) gives the theme's values and the defaults for the parts it does not set, and copies light / dark, behaviour and the desktop background from the preferences. `model::resolve_selected` replaces `apply_icon_set`; a theme never touches `DesktopSettings` any more. `settings.json` keeps the resolved look as the runtime cache (the hub is unchanged).
- `theme.json` drops the `behavior`, `desktop` and `background` sections (old files still parse, the values are ignored); `appearance.theme_mode` in a theme is ignored. `ThemeDraft` loses the same fields; `ThemeDesktopMetrics` is gone.
- Wallpapers in a theme: new `wallpapers/` folder (`themes::WALLPAPERS_DIR`), `themes::theme_wallpapers` (sorted pictures plus the picture a legacy `background.image` names, `themes::legacy_wallpaper`), `AssetOp::AddWallpaper` / `RemoveWallpaper`, `draft::queue_remove_wallpaper`. The writer carries `wallpapers/` and copies a legacy desktop picture into it; Import keeps only pictures in it. The Theme Editor Pictures tab shows a WALLPAPERS list instead of DESKTOP BACKGROUND.
- Settings window: "From theme..." next to "Browse..." in BACKGROUND (`BackgroundSection::connect_theme_pictures`, a thumbnail chooser of the selected theme's wallpapers). The Lister tab keeps only BEHAVIOUR (PRESET, COLOURS and APPEARANCE moved to the Theme Editor); "Save theme..." is removed (Duplicate / Save as copy in the editor cover it); the picker is headed THEME and the light / dark selector LIGHT / DARK; "Edit theme..." is now "Edit themes...".
- Theme Editor: the Colours tab no longer offers light / dark; the default theme (`breeze-dark`) is read only (`writer::is_read_only`, `write_theme` refuses it, the editor says to duplicate it).
- Migration: `Settings.look_version` (`CURRENT_LOOK_VERSION` = 1, missing reads 0). On start `opusdm-settings` runs `migrate::migrate_look`: when the look in an older `settings.json` differs from the active theme it is saved as a new theme "My Theme" (`my-theme`, `my-theme-2`, ...; pictures that no longer exist are dropped) carrying the active theme's icons, fonts, sounds and wallpapers, and selected; the result is persisted through `apply_full` and reported in the note line.
- Docs: `OpusDM-Features-Guide.md` section 19 and `FEATURES.md` (F-35) updated.

## 2026-10-01

- Docs: new `OPTIMIZE.md`, a prioritised list of 19 optimisation and quality items (2 critical, 6 high, 6 medium, 5 low) found in a code review.
- Fix: Enter in the Command Palette did not run the selected command. The entry's inner text widget consumed the key before the bubble-phase controller saw it; the controller now runs in the capture phase (`open_command_palette` in `lister/src/palette.rs`).
- Feature: "Size Format..." command (View, no default key) sets how this Lister writes the Size column: Auto (default, binary `1.5K`), decimal units (`1.5kB`), exact bytes (`1,234,567 B`), or always KB / MB / GB with two decimals. Per Lister, session only. `model::SizeFormat` and `format_size_as` (`format_size` is unchanged, used by the viewer, preview and tools), `Ui.view.size_format`, new `size_format.rs` requester.
- Fix: right clicking Copy / Move opened the "As..." dialog on button press, so the modal dialog swallowed the release and the button stayed pressed after Cancel. The dialog now opens on release (`wire_transfer` in `lister/src/actions.rs`).
- Release: version bumped to 0.5.0 (`opusdm_ui::VERSION` shown in the About requester, every crate and `Cargo.lock`).
- Docs: `OpusDM-Features-Guide.md` updated with the new features: Root/Parent variants and bookmarks (3.7, 3.8), Copy As/Move As on several items, rename preselection, Encrypt/Decrypt (7.10), FTP/FTPS/SFTP (11.3), size reuse and force rescan, View Font (12.4), Confirm, Run, Change Directory options, function conflicts, busy indicator and Command Line (15.4 to 15.8), File Types (16.5), full themes and the Theme Editor (19.6); the "not available yet" list follows `TODO.md`.
- Docs: new `OpusDM-Features-Guide.md`, a step by step tour in Italian of every OpusDM feature (setup, Lister, SOURCE/DEST, file operations, search, viewer, terminal, archives, Functions, hotkeys, user menu, script events, desktop, Button Banks, settings), with the keys to press and where to click.
- Docs: `TODO.md` reorganised by priority: to do now (F-06, conflict prompt UI, F-39, F-14), to do later, not classified, will not be done (F-25, F-26, F-27, F-47) and completed.
- Feature (F-35): full themes. New `opusdm_ipc::look` types (`FontSpec`, `Fonts`, `SurfaceImage`, `Surfaces`, `ThemeSurface`, `ThemeSurfaces`), `Settings.fonts` / `Settings.surfaces` (defaulted, an old `settings.json` still loads) and optional `fonts` / `surfaces` sections in `theme.json`; image names are validated with `is_plain_file_name`. Applying a theme merges per section: `Fonts` replaces the whole value (a theme that sets only `list` resets `ui` to the default, like colours), surfaces merge per surface. `opusdm_ui::look_css` (font and picture CSS; fits are the four existing `fill` / `fit` / `center` / `tile`; file rows are drawn at `ROW_ALPHA_WITH_PICTURE`, 0.6, when the Lister has a picture) and `opusdm_ui::fonts` (fontconfig FFI, `register_theme_fonts`, a registry that remembers accepted and rejected files). The Lister applies the list font and picture and registers the theme fonts; requesters get the picture through the `opus-requester` class (set by `opusdm-ui` requester and `DialogShell` windows), but `ui_look_css` (UI font and requester picture) is loaded only by the Lister and the Settings window, so only requesters shown by those two processes get it; Hub dialogs, the panel, the dock and Button Banks do not load it; the Settings window registers the active theme fonts at startup; the Theme Editor does not register fonts itself.
- Feature (F-35): new `opusdm-themepack` crate, the zip packer shared by `xtask package-themes` and "Export theme...". `writer::write_theme` (staging folder and swap, the old theme is renamed to `.<slug>.old` and restored if the second rename fails) is shared by "Save theme..." and the Theme Editor. "Save theme..." (no author field) and "Export theme..." buttons in `opusdm-settings`; import now drops files that are not fonts (`.ttf` / `.otf`) under `fonts/` or not pictures under `backgrounds/`. No `sounds` key is written to `theme.json`: `sounds/` is copied by Save, Export and Import but never played (F-36).
- Feature (F-35): Theme Editor window ("Edit theme..." in `opusdm-settings`, `crates/theme-config/src/editor/`, `draft.rs`): theme list with New, Duplicate, Delete; Save, Save as copy, Revert, Save & Apply (saves, then applies through `apply_full`); a "Set by this theme" checkbox per section (off leaves the field out of `theme.json`); tabs General (label, author, version; `preview.png` is not edited, it is carried over unchanged by every save and a chooser is deferred), Colours (with Appearance), Fonts, Pictures (desktop, Lister, requester), Icons, Sounds. Behaviour and desktop metrics are not edited, they are carried through unchanged.
- Verification (F-35). Covered by unit tests: the `look` types and old-shape parsing (`theme.json`, `settings.json`), the per-section merge, CSS for fonts and every fit mode, the font registry (accepted and rejected files), the writer (staging, swap, failed swap keeps the old theme, asset operations), `build` from the current settings, export, import filtering, `ThemeDraft` load / save / "Set by this theme" / New / Duplicate / Delete, the packer, and the Save, Export, wipe, Import round trip. Only compile-checked, NOT run by hand (no agent could drive the GUI; the manual checklists of Task 7 and Task 11 in `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-10-01-full-themes.md` are still to do): the Save / Export / Edit buttons, the whole Theme Editor window, Lister and requester picture rendering, runtime font registration and the pango refresh, and "Save & Apply".
- Fix (F-35): symlinks are dropped on import (from `fonts/` and `backgrounds/`, and tar symlink/hardlink entries are not unpacked) and never followed on save or export (`writer` copy, `opusdm_themepack`); `preview.png` is carried over by the writer; slugs are validated in `load_draft`, `delete_theme` and `source_slug`; "Save theme..." over an existing theme keeps its author and version.
- Known limitations (F-35): several editor windows can be opened on the same theme; a crash between the two renames of the swap leaves `.<slug>.old` beside a missing theme (recoverable by hand).

## 2026-09-30

- Button Banks (F-02): free-floating windows of buttons. New `opusdm_ipc::buttonbank` (`ButtonBank`, `Btn`, `Banks`; one file per bank under `~/.config/opusdm/buttons/`, `columns` clamped 1..=32, at most 256 buttons, a file that fails to parse is copied to `.bak` and never overwritten, `create_in_dir` never reuses an id whose file exists, a saved position that is negative or beyond 32000 is dropped) and the new `opusdm-buttonbank` crate: `window.rs` (one window per bank, grid of buttons with icon and label, left / right / middle click each run their own Function, a button whose Functions were all deleted is insensitive with a tooltip, kept out of the taskbar and pager with `_NET_WM_STATE_SKIP_TASKBAR` / `_NET_WM_STATE_SKIP_PAGER`, moved to its saved frame position once the window manager has adopted the window, retried until it sticks; a saved position no connected monitor shows is dropped, and a hidden bank comes back where it was) and `editor.rs` (manager with New, Edit, Delete, Open, Close; editor with preview, Add, Insert, Delete, Copy, Paste, Up, Down, button form and bank appearance). New internal commands `OpenBank`, `CloseBank`, `ToggleBank`, `HideBank` (parameter `bank`, picked from a drop-down of the saved banks in the Function Editor; an unknown bank fails the step with `Bank "<name>" not found`), routed as `FnEvent::Bank` to `HubEvent::Bank`. The hub owns the windows (`crates/hub/src/bank_windows.rs`): banks open when the hub last ran are restored at startup, a click runs the Function through `HubCmd::RunFunctionHeadless`, the position is saved when a window closes and before the hub quits, `Hide` / `Reveal` hide and show the bank windows too. The Function Editor keeps a bank name that no longer exists as "(missing)" instead of rewriting the step. The manager opens from "Button Banks..." in the desktop menu and in the Command Palette (`ToHub::OpenBankManager`).
- Hide and Reveal (F-38): one action hides the environment and leaves the desktop. New internal commands `Hide`, `Reveal` and `Toggle Hide` (no parameters), so a saved function bound to a global hotkey toggles it, and a "Hide All" / "Reveal" button in the desktop menu. The hub owns the state (`hidden`, starts revealed): `ToLister::SetHidden` makes every Lister minimize itself (or restore itself, only if the hub minimized it: `opusdm-lister` `hidden::next_step`), `HubEvent::HiddenChanged` hides the panel, the dock and the group windows (`opusdm_panel::WINDOW_TITLE`, `opusdm_dock::WINDOW_TITLE`). A Lister somebody asked for (`SpawnRequested` / `ToHub::SpawnLister`) opening while hidden reveals everything; the initial one of a `Startup` script is told to hide too (`visible_spawns_pending`). On reveal the Listers restore without raising and the hub presents only the SOURCE Lister, so roles do not shuffle. `opusdm_panel::set_hidden` keeps a panel bar rebuilt on a monitor change hidden.
- Program Groups (F-34): a group is a named collection of programs, files or folders shown as one icon on the desktop. New store `opusdm_ipc::Groups` (`~/.config/opusdm/groups/<id>.json`, one file per group: name, members, optional dragged position; ids are slugs made unique with a numeric suffix, names are unique ignoring case; malformed files are skipped and never deleted). `IconEntry.group` with its own badge and a virtual `group:<id>` path pinned like left-out icons; `opusdm_hub::leftout::DesktopExtras` merges left-out icons and groups into the desktop listing; `launch_action` decides how a member starts (folder in a Lister, `.desktop` application, program with an ELF or `#!` header, default application). `Groups::create_in_dir` never reuses an id whose file already exists on disk, so a malformed group file is not overwritten. New `group_window.rs`: one window per open group with an icon field, double click launches, dropping files adds, context menu "Remove from Group". Desktop: "New Group..." in the desktop menu, dropping files on a group icon adds them, group icon menu with Open, Rename Group... and Delete Group... (members are never touched).
- LeaveOut (F-33): files and folders can be left out on the desktop as permanent icons with an arrow badge. New store `opusdm_ipc::LeftOut` (`~/.config/opusdm/leftout.json`: path plus optional dragged position; duplicates and bad coordinates are cleaned on load), `IconEntry.left_out` and `IconItem` badge, `IconField::set_pinned_positions` / `connect_pinned_moved` (positions kept by path across reloads, only for left-out icons), `scan::entry_for_path`, and `opusdm_hub::leftout::merge_entries` (a path also in the Desktop folder shows once; a missing target stays as a broken icon). Entry points: Lister context menu "Leave Out on Desktop" (`ToHub::LeaveOut`), function steps `Leave Out` and `Put Away` on the SOURCE selection, and "Put Away" in the desktop icon menu. The hub emits `HubEvent::LeftOutAdd` / `LeftOutRemove`; the GTK side owns and saves the store.; `docs/usage.md` 3.12 no longer claims a `.opusdm/` layout directory. Desktop reloads share one latest-wins gate (`opusdm_hub::leftout::ReloadGate`), a reload cancels a drag in progress, a left-out symlink to a folder opens in a lister, and Cut, Rename and Delete are not offered on left-out icons.
- Functions: new internal command "Set Background" (`InternalCmd::SetBackground`, required `path` with placeholders, `~` and cwd-relative paths, optional `fit` = fill / fit / center / tile, empty keeps the current fit). The step checks the file exists and has a png / jpg / jpeg / webp / bmp / gif extension, then the hub sets `theme.background` to that image and applies it through the same path as `ToHub::SetSettings` (now `Hub::apply_settings`): persisted to `settings.json`, live redraw. Hub-originated runs (script events) apply it too. New helpers `ImageFit::parse` and `is_background_image` in `opusdm_ipc::desktop`.
- Lister: right-click an image file, "Set as Desktop Background" (refused for paths containing `{`).
- Docs: `docs/usage.md` section 3.20 describes the Change Directory and Set Background function steps.

## 2026-09-29

- Functions: new internal command "Change Directory" (`InternalCmd::ChangeDir`, required `path` parameter with `{f}`/`{o}`/... placeholders, `~` and paths relative to the current directory). The hub validates that the target is an existing directory, sends `ToLister::GoTo` to the SOURCE Lister and runs every later step from the new directory.
- Lister nav bar: Back, Forward and History buttons now use SVG icons (`arrow-left.svg`, `arrow-right.svg`, `chevron-down.svg`) like Home and Parent, instead of the Unicode triangle glyphs.

"Reset Default" asks for confirmation through the same `opusdm_ui::requester` shape as the Lister's delete confirmation, then discards the tree and reseeds it. Each app row now shows its resolved icon, not just its name.

- Renamed the `opusdm-theme` app to `opusdm-settings` (binary name, `application_id`, window title, and every source comment naming it). The `opusdm-theme-config` crate/package name is unchanged.
- Editable application menu: new `opusdm_ipc::menu` (`MenuConfig`/`MenuItem`, a tree of folders and apps persisted to `~/.config/opusdm/menu.json`; pure `add_app`/`add_folder`/`rename_folder`/`remove_at`/`move_up`/`move_down` operate on a `path: &[usize]`). `opusdm-panel`'s application menu now renders this tree (folders as nested expanders); on first run, or whenever the file is emptied, it is seeded from the installed apps grouped by freedesktop category (one folder per category) and the seed is written back to `menu.json`, mirroring how `opusdm-dock` seeds `dock.json`. New "Menu" tab in `opusdm-settings` (`crates/desktop-config/src/menu_ui.rs`, `MenuSection`): a tree editor with per-row move up/down/remove, per-folder add-app/add-folder/rename, "Add App..." (file chooser starting at the user's home) and "Add Folder..." for the root.

## 2026-09-27

- Fix: the dock stayed on top of maximised windows despite `_NET_WM_STATE_BELOW`. Cause: it used `_NET_WM_WINDOW_TYPE_DOCK`, which by EWMH spec tells the window manager to always keep the window raised - the same type the panel uses, for the opposite reason. Switched to `_NET_WM_WINDOW_TYPE_NORMAL` (still undecorated, still no strut) plus `SKIP_TASKBAR`/`SKIP_PAGER` so it stays out of the taskbar and pager; `BELOW` now actually keeps it under normal windows.
- `opusdm-dock` step 5, and the final part of step 4: new `crates/desktop-config/src/dock_ui.rs` with `DockSection` (edge drop-down limited to the edges free of the panel, icon size 32/48/64) and `DockAppsSection` (ordered list of pinned apps with up/down/remove/"Add..." picking a `.desktop` file), reused by a new "Dock" tab in `opusdm-theme`. `PanelSection::connect_position_changed` lets the Panel tab push its edge to the Dock tab live, so the two never offer the same edge. `opusdm-dock` itself gained the rest of step 4: drag-reorder inside the dock (`gtk::DragSource`/`DropTarget` on `String`), dropping a `.desktop` file (or a file list of them) onto the dock to pin it, dragging an icon out to unpin it, and popover context menus (an icon's own `Actions=` entries plus "Remove from Dock"; the background's "Add Application..."). New pure `crates/dock/src/list.rs` (`insert_at`/`remove`) shared by both crates' reordering.
- `docs/dock.md` implementation plan is now a status list: all five steps are done.
- `opusdm-dock` steps 2 and 3 of `docs/dock.md`: the dock now shows the pinned apps from `~/.config/opusdm/dock.json` (new `opusdm_ipc::DockConfig`; a missing file is seeded with file manager, terminal and browser, keeping the installed ones), with the app icon, name tooltip, click-to-launch through `gio::DesktopAppInfo` + the display's launch context, and a short busy cursor. The dock watches `dock.json` with a `gio::FileMonitor` and follows edits live; uninstalled ids are skipped.
- New `opusdm_ipc::DockPosition` (bottom default, top, left, right) with `avoiding(PanelPosition)`, plus `DesktopSettings::dock_position` and `dock_icon_size` (default 48, clamped to `DOCK_ICON_SIZE_RANGE`). The dock never shares the panel's edge (it jumps to the opposite one) and a left/right dock is centered in the strip the panel leaves free (`Placement::rect`).
- `opusdm_dock::attach(app, DockLayout)` returns a setter; the hub spawns the dock next to the panel and calls the setter on `DesktopSettingsChanged`, so edge, icon size and panel edge apply live. The dock follows monitor hotplug.
- New crate `crates/dock` (`opusdm-dock`), step 1 of `docs/dock.md`: a `DOCK`-type window with `_NET_WM_STATE_BELOW` and no strut, so maximised windows cover it. `placement.rs` holds the pure geometry (`DockEdge`, `Placement::rect`, unit-tested; all four edges, default bottom, centered on the primary monitor). `window.rs` applies the X11 hints. `attach(app)` shows a static row of three placeholder themed icons; `main.rs` runs it standalone. Not spawned by the hub yet. The spec now says square corners, since rounding needs transparency.
- New design document `docs/dock.md` for `opusdm-dock`, a launcher-only dock (no strut, opaque, no zoom, no window list, never on the panel's edge). No code yet.
- Removed every Nerd Font dependency: deleted `iconview::glyphs` and `iconview::font_probe`, the "Nerd Font Not Found" startup dialog, the Nerd Font CSS and `scripts/install-font.sh`. Icons now always come from an installed theme; a theme lacking an icon leaves a blank slot. `IconSetId::None` is gone (`IconSetId` is now `Custom(slug)` only, default `breeze-dark`); legacy `"None"`/`"BreezeDark"` values in `settings.json` map to the default. The `breeze-dark` theme is embedded in `opusdm-ipc` and written to `~/.config/opusdm/themes/` by `themes::ensure_default_theme()` whenever it is missing (called by `scan_themes` and the icon loader). `decorate_name` in the Lister lost its glyph parameter.
- Desktop context menu: the "Theme" entry is now called "Settings" (it still launches `opusdm-theme`); the missing-font dialog hint follows.
- New "Panel" setting in the Desktop tab of `opusdm-theme`: choose whether the panel is docked to the top (default) or the bottom of the screen. New `opusdm_ipc::PanelPosition` and `DesktopSettings::panel_position` (`"top"`/`"bottom"` in `desktop.json`; missing field means top), edited through the new `PanelSection` in `crates/desktop-config/src/ui.rs`.
- Panel (`crates/panel`): new `window::Placement` (edge + monitor rect). A bottom bar sits at `mon_y + mon_h - PANEL_HEIGHT` and reserves its space with the bottom slots of `_NET_WM_STRUT_PARTIAL` (including the gap between the monitor and the screen bottom on multi-head setups). `strut_partial` now takes the position.
- The application menu popover opens upwards when the panel is at the bottom (`appmenu::set_direction` flips the `MenuButton` arrow direction).
- `opusdm_panel::attach(app, position)` now returns a setter; the hub calls it on `DesktopSettingsChanged`, so the panel moves live without a restart. Bars are re-docked in place (`redock_bar`) rather than rebuilt, so the primary bar keeps its tray and SNI widgets.
- The desktop icon field keeps its clearance margin on the panel's edge (`keep_clear_of_panel` in `toolbar.rs`): top margin for a top panel, bottom margin for a bottom one.

## 2026-09-13

- Lister clipboard: Ctrl+C / Ctrl+X / Ctrl+V. New module `crates/lister/src/clip.rs`: Copy/Cut writes the selection to the system text clipboard as `text/uri-list` (reusing `opusdm_iconview::dnd::paths_to_uri_list`) with a leading `#opusdm-clip:cut`/`copy` marker line; Paste reads it back (`uris_to_paths`) and sends `ToHub::RunJob{op, srcs, dest_dir: Some(cwd)}`, the same path already used by drag-and-drop and the Source->Dest toolbar buttons. Works within one Lister, across two Lister instances, and pasting into a plain directory, since the OS clipboard already crosses process boundaries - no new hub/IPC message needed. ## 2026-09-09

- Nuovo modulo `crates/ui/src/version.rs` con la sola costante `opusdm_ui::VERSION` (default `"0.1.0"`), pensato per essere editato a mano quando si cambia versione. Il requester About (sia quello dell'hub in `toolbar.rs::show_about_dialog` sia quello del pannello in `appmenu.rs::show_about`) ora costruisce il markup con `format!` e mostra una riga `Version {VERSION}` sotto il sottotitolo.

- Overlay di debug della griglia icone. Con `GRID_DEBUG=1` nell'ambiente, `opusdm_iconview::style::install` carica un secondo `CssProvider` a priorita' `APPLICATION + 1` che disegna un `outline` rosso attorno a ogni `.icon-item` (piu' un contorno rosso semitrasparente su glifo ed etichetta), cosi' si vedono gli ingombri reali senza alterare il layout. Vale sia per il campo icone del desktop dell'hub sia per ogni Lister. Nuovo flag `--grid-debug` in `run-x11.sh` che esporta `GRID_DEBUG=1` prima di lanciare l'hub.

- `IconItem`: il `gtk::Entry` di rinomina inline non e' piu' figlio permanente dello `Stack` del nome. Un `Entry` chiede sempre spazio per `max_width_chars` e uno `Stack` misura il piu' grande dei suoi figli anche nascosti, quindi ogni icona veniva misurata ~130px di larghezza a prescindere dal nome; tramite il passo max-of-all di `FreeArrange` questo distanziava tutta la griglia del desktop. Ora l'entry viene aggiunto in `start_edit` e rimosso in `finish_edit`: a riposo l'item e' largo quanto icona + label.
- Il campo icone del desktop parte sotto il pannello: `build_desktop_area` mette un `margin_top` pari a `opusdm_panel::PANEL_HEIGHT` sul widget dell'`IconField`, cosi' la prima riga di icone non finisce piu' nascosta dietro il dock (lo sfondo continua a coprire tutto il monitor).
- `scan::read_entries` ordina il risultato (directory prima, poi file, ogni blocco per nome case-insensitive) invece di restituire l'ordine grezzo di `read_dir`. Tre dei quattro punti dell'hub che popolano il desktop non chiamavano `sort_dirs_first` e la griglia usciva disordinata; ora l'ordine e' garantito alla fonte e la chiamata esplicita in "Rearrange icons" e' ridondante (rimossa). Il Lister non e' toccato: la sua vista a icone si ordina gia' da sola sul modello.
- Passo della griglia del desktop configurabile e non piu' dettato dalla label piu' larga. `FreeArrange` perde il termine `max_w`/`max_h` e la costante `SLOT_GAP`: il passo e' `cell.max(min_cell) + gap` uguale sui due assi, dove `cell` = `grid_spacing`, `gap` = nuovo `grid_gap`, `min_cell` = `icon_size` (pavimento perche' i glifi non si sovrappongano mai). Label piu' larghe del passo si sovrappongono: e' la scelta esplicita di chi stringe la griglia. Nuovo campo `DesktopSettings::grid_gap` (px, default 4, `GRID_GAP_RANGE` 0..64); `GRID_SPACING_RANGE` abbassato a 24..320. `opus-desktop-config` mostra una terza riga "Grid gap" nella sezione ICON GRID; l'hub applica `grid_gap` all'avvio e sul live-update. Nuovi setter `IconField::set_grid_gap`.

- Nuovo crate `crates/panel` (`opusdm_panel`): pannello stile KDE Plasma, una seconda finestra `_NET_WM_WINDOW_TYPE_DOCK` ancorata in alto sul monitor primario, alta 40px, con `_NET_WM_STRUT_PARTIAL` in modo che le finestre massimizzate non ci finiscano sotto. Vive sulla stessa `gtk::Application` dell'hub, nessun IPC nuovo: `opusdm_panel::attach(app, PanelHooks { on_settings })` la costruisce e la presenta. Design in `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-09-09-panel-bar-design.md`.
- Contenuto del pannello. Sinistra: menu applicazioni (`gtk::MenuButton` + popover), pager dei virtual desktop, window list del desktop corrente. Destra: CPU (sparkline con la percentuale dentro, in alto a destra), rete (sparkline a due tracce + testo down/up separato), orologio a due righe (ora grande, data piccola locale).
- Menu applicazioni (`appmenu.rs`): legge i `.desktop` installati via crate `freedesktop-desktop-entry` (`default-features = false`, niente gettext), filtra `NoDisplay`/`Hidden`/non-Application, raggruppa per categoria freedesktop in 10 bucket a ordine fisso (Development, Graphics, Internet, Multimedia, Office, Games, System, Settings, Utility, Other), un `gtk::Expander` per bucket con icone dal tema. Sotto: Settings (riusa il dialog dell'hub via hook), About, Logout (`openbox --exit`), Shutdown (`systemctl poweroff`), gli ultimi due con conferma `opusdm_ui::requester`. Lancio via `gio::DesktopAppInfo`.
- Osservatore EWMH (`ewmh.rs`): thread dedicato con connessione `x11rb` propria, `PropertyChangeMask` sulla root, traduce i cambi di `_NET_CURRENT_DESKTOP` / `_NET_NUMBER_OF_DESKTOPS` / `_NET_DESKTOP_NAMES` / `_NET_CLIENT_LIST` / `_NET_ACTIVE_WINDOW` (e title/state delle finestre client) in `EwmhEvent` su un `async-channel` drenato dal loop GTK - stesso schema di `server.rs`. Azioni click (`switch_desktop`, `activate_window`, `iconify_window`) via `ClientMessage` con connessione usa-e-getta. Parser puri testati: `parse_desktop_names`, `parse_window_list`, `pick_icon` (da `_NET_WM_ICON` ARGB a RGBA8).
- Pager (`widgets/pager.rs`): un `gtk::Button` per desktop numerato `1..=N`, quello corrente con classe `.active`, click = `_NET_CURRENT_DESKTOP`, tooltip col nome. Ricostruito solo quando cambia il numero di desktop.
- Window list (`widgets/tasklist.rs`): un pulsante icona+titolo per ogni finestra del desktop corrente (piu le omnipresenti), attiva evidenziata, minimizzata in `.dim-label`. Click = attiva; click sulla finestra attiva e visibile = `WM_CHANGE_STATE` IconicState. Icona da `_NET_WM_ICON` (`gdk::MemoryTexture`) con fallback `application-x-executable`. Ricostruita per intero a ogni snapshot.
- Monitor di sistema (`widgets/sysmon.rs`): campionamento a 1s di `/proc/stat` e `/proc/net/dev`, ring buffer di 60 campioni, sparkline disegnate con Cairo (CPU una traccia scala 0-100, rete due tracce down/up scala automatica). Funzioni pure testate: `parse_proc_stat`, `cpu_percent`, `parse_net_dev`, `human_rate`.
- Hub: rimossa la striscia toolbar dalla finestra desktop. Via New Lister, indicatori SRC/DST, box di avanzamento job, pulsanti Settings/About/Quit dalla chrome dell'hub; `Widgets` perde `src_label`/`dst_label`/`src_id`/`dst_id`/`job_box`/`jobs` e `JobRow` sparisce. La finestra desktop e ora solo sfondo + campo icone + menu contestuale (che tiene ancora New Lister / Settings / About / Desktop / Quit). `apply_event` ignora `Roles`/`JobUpdate`/`JobFinished` (il server li emette ancora, il Lister richiedente mostra il proprio avanzamento). `build_ui` chiama `opusdm_panel::attach` dopo aver presentato la finestra desktop e ri-ancora la geometria del desktop a 600/1600ms per riprendersi i 40px che lo strut del pannello toglie all'area di lavoro.
- Il pannello ri-asserisce tipo finestra, strut, geometria e stacking a 80/400/1000ms dopo il map, per non restare sotto quando il WM applica la sua policy di posizionamento o la finestra desktop dell'hub assesta la propria geometria. Nuovo `crates/panel/examples/standalone.rs` per provarlo isolato.

## 2026-09-08

- Nuova app `opus-desktop-config` (crate `crates/desktop-config`, binario `opus-desktop-config`): pannello di configurazione del desktop con tre sezioni - sfondo, griglia icone, cartella desktop. La lancia la voce "Desktop -> Settings" del menu contestuale del desktro (`launch_desktop_config` risolve il binario accanto a quello dell'hub, fallback su `PATH`). Pulsanti Cancel / Apply (invia senza chiudere) / Save (invia e chiude).
- Sfondo del desktop configurabile. Nuovo `DesktopSettings` in `crates/ipc/src/desktop.rs` (formato del file, stato in memoria dell'hub e payload IPC, stessa logica di `Settings`): `background` con modo esplicito `Solid` / `Gradient` / `Image`, colore solido, due colori + angolo 0-360 per il gradiente, path immagine + fit `Fill` / `Fit` / `Center` / `Tile`; piu `icon_size`, `grid_spacing` e `desktop_dir` opzionale. Tutti i campi `#[serde(default)]` e clampati da `DesktopSettings::sanitized`. Default identici all'aspetto attuale (`#2e3436`, icone 64px, passo 96px).
- L'hub rende lo sfondo rigenerando il CSS: `install_desktop_css` ora prende `&Background`, con l'helper puro `opusdm_ipc::background_css` (Solid -> `background`, Gradient -> `linear-gradient(<deg>, a, b)`, Image -> `background-image: url("file://...")` con `background-size`/`-repeat` per il fit; `Image` senza path valido ricade sul colore solido). `path_to_file_url` percent-codifica il path.
- Config su file: nuovo `~/.config/opusdm/desktop.json`, gemello di `settings.json`, con `crates/hub/src/desktop_settings.rs` per load/save. L'hub e l'unico scrittore quando e in esecuzione; `opus-desktop-config` scrive il file direttamente solo se l'hub non gira (nota "prossimo avvio" nella finestra).
- IPC: nuovo `ToHub::SetDesktopSettings(DesktopSettings)` (inviato da `opus-desktop-config`, non da un Lister) e `HubEvent::DesktopSettingsChanged`. L'hub sanifica, persiste e applica dal vivo: ridisegna lo sfondo, applica `set_icon_size` + il nuovo `IconField::set_grid_spacing` al campo del desktop, e se `desktop_dir` cambia ri-risolve la cartella (`desktop_files::effective_desktop_dir`, override valido -> XDG -> `~/Desktop`), ricrea il `DesktopWatch` (`spawn_desktop_watch`) e ricarica le icone.
- `IconField::set_grid_spacing(px)` imposta il passo di auto-slot di `FreeArrange` (prima fisso a 96 in `FreeArrange::new`). `Widgets.desktop_dir` passa a `RefCell<Option<PathBuf>>`; il drop sul desktop legge la cartella corrente invece di una copia catturata alla partenza.
- Stato per-cartella: `crates/iconview/src/scan.rs` non usa piu il file sciolto `.opusdm-layout` ma una directory `.opusdm/` per cartella (`OPUSDM_DIR`, `LAYOUT_BASENAME`, `layout_path(dir) -> <dir>/.opusdm/layout`); `read_entries` la nasconde sempre, il Lister la filtra dalla lista. Cosi altri file di OpusDM legati alla cartella avranno un posto unico.
- Limite noto: se all'avvio non c'e una cartella desktop, cambiarla da `opus-desktop-config` non ricrea il campo icone finche l'hub non viene riavviato.

## 2026-09-07

- Fix `IconItem` / `FreeArrange`: le icone del desktop si ri-distanziavano dopo un click. Sequenza: al primo caricamento la griglia e larga, "Rearrange icons" la compatta, poi un click su un'icona (o "in mezzo", perche con il passo gonfiato i rettangoli degli item quasi si toccano) la ri-allarga. Causa: la thumbnail era una `gtk::Picture`, che riporta come dimensione naturale quella intrinseca della texture; una thumbnail verticale (foto in portrait, pagina PDF) faceva crescere l'item da ~114px a ~217px di altezza. Le thumbnail arrivano in modo asincrono dopo `set_entries`, ma il `gtk::Fixed` non rifluisce da solo, quindi il grid resta compatto finche un `relayout` non gira di nuovo: `end_reposition` ne lancia uno anche sul click sotto-soglia, e a quel punto `FreeArrange` ricava `pitch_y` dal `max()` degli item (thumbnail incluse) e sparpaglia tutte le righe. Ora la thumbnail e una `gtk::Image` con `pixel_size` pari alla dimensione dell'icona: misura sempre un quadrato `icon_size` e ci scala dentro la texture, quindi un item con thumbnail misura come uno con glyph e il `relayout` e stabile.
- Fix `IconItem` / `FreeArrange`: sul desktro la griglia delle icone risultava larghissima (colonne distanti ~425px invece di ~150px). Il `name_label` va a capo in modalita `Word`, quindi un nome file senza spazi ne trattini (per es. `726432604_122131481643172103_8689873794089316539_n.jpg`) e un'unica parola non spezzabile: `max_width_chars(14)` viene ignorato e la label richiede la sua larghezza a riga singola (~410px). `FreeArrange` ricava il passo delle colonne dal `max()` dei box degli item, quindi quel singolo item allargava ogni colonna. Ora `name_label` usa `WrapMode::WordChar` e spezza anche dentro il token: ogni `IconItem` misura ~142px a prescindere dal nome.
- Fix `IconField` (desktop / modalita Free): il doppio click a volte attivava l'icona sbagliata. `FreeArrange` disponeva le icone auto-slottate su una griglia a passo fisso di 96px mentre un `IconItem` misura ~130x105px, quindi i rettangoli in `item_rects` si sovrapponevano e `item_at` (primo match) risolveva i click nella cucitura sull'icona di indice piu basso; sul doppio click i due `pressed` potevano cadere ai due lati della cucitura. Il passo ora e `max(box item + gap, cell)` come in `GridArrange`; `item_at` scandisce anche dall'ultima alla prima cosi un mucchio di icone trascinate una sull'altra colpisce quella in cima.
- Fix `IconField`: il drag di riposizionamento delle icone sul desktop non funzionava (l'icona tornava al punto di partenza). Sul `Fixed` c'erano due gesture sul tasto sinistro, un `DragSource` per il drag inter-finestra e un `GestureDrag` per il riposizionamento; in Free mode il `DragSource` restituiva il payload uri-list per primo e GTK avviava un drag-and-drop che rubava la gesture. Ora il `DragSource` resta inerte in Free mode (`inter_window_drag_enabled`) e il riposizionamento ha `drag-update` con feedback dal vivo, committando le posizioni in pixel su `drag-end`. `apply_reposition` sostituita da `begin`/`update`/`end_reposition`.
- Hub: sottomenu "Desktop" nel menu contestuale del desktop (`gtk::Expander`), con "Rearrange icons" (rilegge la cartella desktop, ordina directory-prima-poi-file per nome via `opusdm_iconview::scan::sort_dirs_first`, e la ripassa al campo che la dispone nella griglia) e "Settings" (segnaposto, per ora chiude solo il menu). `Widgets` tiene un handle all'`IconField` del desktop e alla sua directory.
- Fase 2 dell'`IconField`: thumbnail di anteprima. Nuovo modulo `crates/iconview/src/thumbs` - cache freedesktop (`~/.cache/thumbnails/<bucket>/<md5 dell'URI>.png`, tag `Thumb::MTime`/`Thumb::URI` scritti e verificati via parser `tEXt` PNG interno con CRC-32), generatori per immagini (gdk-pixbuf in-process, nessun tool esterno), PDF (`pdftoppm` -> `pdftocairo`) e video (`ffmpegthumbnailer` -> `ffmpeg`, shell-out con timeout di 20s e kill), e un pool di worker a dimensione fissa (max 4) modellato su `opusdm_fsops::pool`. L'`IconField` accoda un job per ogni voce anteprimabile su `set_entries` (e sul cambio di bucket in `set_icon_size`), applica i risultati sul main loop creando la `gdk::Texture` dai byte PNG, con guardia di generazione e confronto del path per scartare i risultati obsoleti. Il glyph Nerd Font resta il fallback e lo stato iniziale; nessun cross-fade. `OPUSDM_NO_THUMBS=1` disattiva tutto. Nuove dipendenze `md-5` (RustCrypto, hash dell'URI richiesto dallo standard) e `async-channel` per il crate. Nessuna modifica ai consumatori (Lister, hub). Il comando `ffmpeg` prende il primo frame senza `-ss` (un seek oltre la fine di una clip molto corta non produceva frame).
- Nuovo crate `crates/iconview` (`opusdm_iconview`): vista a icone riutilizzabile (fase 1, walking skeleton). `IconField` e un `gtk::Fixed` in uno `ScrolledWindow` con un trait interno `Arrange` (`GridArrange` per il Lister, `FreeArrange` per il desktop). Selezione multipla, rubber-band, navigazione da tastiera, rename inline, zoom Ctrl+scroll e drag and drop (uri-list tra finestre + riposizionamento locale in modalita Free). Callback invece di segnali GObject. Hit-test e navigazione leggono i rettangoli calcolati in `relayout()`, non la geometria GTK. Nessuna dipendenza di terze parti nuova.
- `glyphs.rs` e il codec `text/uri-list` di `dnd.rs` spostati dal Lister a `opusdm_iconview`; `glyph_for` generalizzato a `glyph_for(kind, mode, name)`. Il Lister re-importa dal crate e tiene un `dnd.rs` ridotto ad adapter (`attach_field`). `crates/lister/src/glyphs.rs` ora contiene solo `decorate_name` + `type_indicator` (basati su `Row`).
- Lister: nuovo toggle "vista a icone" nella button row; uno `gtk::Stack` alterna `ColumnView` ("details") e `IconField` ("icons"). In modalita icone gli `IconEntry` sono derivati dai `Row` gia ordinati (`model::row_to_entry`), senza rileggere la directory. `activate`/`rename`/`context-menu`/`zoom`/`drop` cablati sugli handler esistenti (`activate_row`, `context_menu::show_context_menu`, `dnd::attach_field`). `.opusdm-layout` filtrato dalla lista.
- Hub: la finestra desktop mostra il contenuto di `$XDG_DESKTOP_DIR` (fallback `~/Desktop`, altrimenti area vuota con la sola `Label` "OpusDM" come prima) in un `IconField` in modalita `Free`. Nuovo `crates/hub/src/desktop_files.rs`: risoluzione della cartella (parsing di `user-dirs.dirs`) + `DesktopWatch`, watch inotify a directory singola con debounce, drenato da un future locale che ricarica le entry. `build_desktop_area()` ora prende la dir e restituisce `(Overlay, Option<IconField>)`. Doppio click su cartella apre un Lister (`HubCmd::SpawnRequested`), sui file usa l'app di default; drop sul desktop lancia un job di copia/spostamento tramite il nuovo `HubCmd::RunJob` (requester sentinella `u64::MAX`, nessun instradamento di progresso verso un Lister). Il menu contestuale del desktop resta invariato.
- Fasi successive: le posizioni delle icone sul desktop non sono ancora persistite fra i riavvii (fase 3, `.opusdm-layout`); le thumbnail di immagini/PDF/video arrivano nella fase 2 (per ora ogni icona e un glyph Nerd Font). Il menu contestuale sull'`IconField` del desktop non e cablato: il tasto destro passa al menu dell'overlay esistente.
- L'hub non e piu una toolbar top-docked ma la finestra desktop del WM: `ApplicationWindow` senza decorazioni a schermo intero sul monitor primario, marcata `_NET_WM_WINDOW_TYPE_DESKTOP` e tenuta sotto ogni altra finestra (`_NET_WM_STATE_BELOW`), ancorata a 0,0. Niente piu `_NET_WM_STRUT`/`_NET_WM_STRUT_PARTIAL`: il desktop e lo sfondo, i Lister ci galleggiano sopra e possono coprirlo (modello Directory Opus).
- `crates/hub/src/dock.rs` rinominato `desktop.rs`: `make_top_dock` -> `make_desktop_window(win, w, h)` (tipo finestra DESKTOP + keep-below + geometria piena, senza strut), `monitor_width` -> `monitor_geometry(display) -> (i32, i32)`, `pin_geometry` su `(w, h)`. `PANEL_HEIGHT` sostituita da `TOOLBAR_HEIGHT` in `toolbar.rs` (solo layout). `mod dock;` -> `mod desktop;` in `main.rs`.
- `toolbar.rs`: la finestra full-screen impagina in verticale la toolbar in alto (widget invariati, altezza `TOOLBAR_HEIGHT`), la job box, e una nuova area desktop `hexpand`/`vexpand` con sfondo a tinta piena (`DESKTOP_BG` via `CssProvider`) e una `Label` "OpusDM" centrata. `Widgets.panel_width: i32` -> `monitor_size: (i32, i32)`.
- `HubEvent::RaiseToolbar` (handoff single-instance): ora ri-asserisce solo tipo finestra e geometria del desktop, senza `present()` che lo porterebbe sopra i Lister; il WM lo tiene comunque in fondo. Messaggi IPC `ToHub::RaiseToolbar`/`HubEvent::RaiseToolbar` invariati.
- Menu contestuale sul desktop (tasto destro): New Lister / --- / Settings / About / --- / Quit. La finestra desktop non riceve mai il focus, quindi un `Popover` ancorato ad essa non riesce a fare il grab e resta invisibile: il menu e un `gtk::Box` posato su un `gtk::Overlay` che ora avvolge l'area desktro, posizionato con i margini al punto del click e ritagliato dentro i bordi. Si chiude cliccando una voce o con click sinistro fuori dal menu. `build_desktop_area()` ora restituisce un `gtk::Overlay`.
- La conferma di uscita e sempre mostrata: `request_quit()` non chiude piu direttamente quando non ci sono job, mostra sempre il requester ("Quit OpusDM?" + "This will close every Lister and the desktop." + eventuale conteggio job). Vale sia per la voce di menu sia per il pulsante Quit della toolbar sia per la chiusura via WM. Nuovo flag `Widgets.quitting` per evitare che il `close()` post-conferma rientri in `request_quit` e impili un secondo dialog.

## 2026-09-04

- Sort cliccando sulle intestazioni di colonna (Name, Size, Date, User, Group, Access), con freccia indicatrice sulla colonna attiva. Gli header sono i pulsanti nativi del `ColumnView`; il `SortListModel` pero non usa il suo sorter, che per il verso discendente invertirebbe l'intero comparatore facendo finire le directory sotto i file. Un `CustomSorter` sul modello legge dal `ColumnViewSorter` solo quale colonna e quale verso sono attivi e li passa a `dir_first_cmp`: doppio blocco, directory sempre in testa, e il verso a->z / z->a inverte solo la chiave dentro ciascun blocco.
- `SortCol` esteso con `User`, `Group`, `Access` e i relativi rami in `dir_first_cmp` (utente e gruppo per nome visualizzato, case-insensitive; access per i bit di `mode`).
- Rimossi il `sort_cell` mai aggiornato e il campo `Ui.sorter`. Il sorter del modello viene svegliato con `changed()` sul segnale `changed` del `ColumnViewSorter` (cambio di colonna/verso da header), non piu dopo ogni `splice`.

- Le requester restituiscono un valore invece di un indice: i bottoni si passano come coppie `(label, valore)` e la callback riceve il valore scelto. `on_conflict` non ha piu un `match` sugli indici e inserire un bottone in mezzo non puo piu dirottare silenziosamente i rami.
- Rete di sicurezza al posto di `deletable(false)`: la requester e di nuovo chiudibile dal WM, ma `connect_close_request` consegna la scelta di cancel. Un unico `deliver()` guardato da un flag serve bottoni, Esc e chiusura WM, cosi `on_choice` gira esattamente una volta e con lo stesso testo dell'entry qualunque sia la via d'uscita - prima una chiusura via WM lasciava l'hub in attesa di `ConflictReply` per sempre.
- Migrati a `opusdm_ui::requester` gli ultimi tre `gtk::AlertDialog`: conferma di uscita dell'hub, delete del menu contestuale e delete della palette. Le tre copie della conferma di delete passano ora da `actions::confirm_delete()`, quindi ordine dei bottoni e default sono gli stessi da qualunque punto d'ingresso (prima la palette e il menu contestuale avevano Cancel come primario, la toolbar no).
- Nuovo `opusdm_ui::DialogShell`: finestra modale + area contenuti + riga bottoni, con margini, spaziature e cablaggio dei bottoni di chiusura in un posto solo. Adottato dai sei dialog che li ricopiavano a mano (errori di job, batch rename, checksum, compare/sync, command palette, settings dell'hub). I margini passano a 12 px uniformi (checksum e compare stavano a 14) e la riga bottoni sparisce del tutto quando il dialog non ne ha (command palette).
- `crates/ui` diviso in `requester.rs` e `shell.rs`; corretto l'em dash nel titolo del Batch Rename.

- CLEANUP - `crates/ui`: un solo parametro `Body { Text | Markup }` al posto della coppia mutuamente esclusiva `message: &str` / `rich_body: Option<Label>`; `build_rich` eliminata e `build` scesa da 8 a 7 parametri.
- Le tre label del requester (corpo, dettaglio, rich) passano da `_body_label()`: la larghezza di wrap vive ora nella costante `BODY_MAX_WIDTH_CHARS` invece di essere ripetuta tre volte.
- Esc ora fa `emit_clicked()` sul bottone di cancel invece di richiamare `on_choice` per conto suo: prima un `requester_entry` chiuso con Esc consegnava una stringa vuota mentre il click sullo stesso bottone consegnava il testo digitato.
- I bottoni chiudono con `destroy()` e non `close()`, rompendo il ciclo dialog -> button -> closure -> dialog che teneva in vita finestra, widget e callback (con dentro `Rc<Ui>`) per tutta la vita del processo.
- Rimosso `add_css_class("requester")`: nessuna regola `.requester` esiste in `theme.rs`, era un aggancio morto.
- `requester_entry` perde il parametro `message`, che nessun chiamante valorizzava. I bottoni non primari usano `Button::with_label` invece di una `Label` figlia costruita a mano, e `build` non e piu generica sul tipo di parent (upcast nei wrapper pubblici): una sola istanza del corpo grande.

## 2026-09-03

- OPTIMIZE 3.6 - Auto-refresh in tempo reale con inotify. Nuovo `crates/lister/src/watcher.rs`: inotify diretto tramite `libc`, senza aggiungere dipendenze. Un thread per Lister possiede il file descriptor e blocca su `poll`; la directory osservata viene sostituita ad ogni navigazione.
- Le raffiche di eventi vengono accorpate (debounce 250 ms, tetto di 2 s): un `cargo build` che scrive migliaia di file provoca un solo reload invece di uno per file. Le notifiche raggiungono il thread GTK su un `async_channel`, lo stesso schema gia usato dal reader dell'hub.
- Nuovo blocco `Behavior { auto_refresh, auto_refresh_debounce_ms }` in `Settings`, con interruttore nella sezione BEHAVIOUR del dialogo Settings. Acceso di default. `debounce_ms()` limita il valore a 50-5000 ms, cosi un settings.json modificato a mano non puo far girare a vuoto il watcher ne farlo sembrare bloccato.
- I path virtuali degli archivi non vengono osservati. Se il kernel rifiuta l'istanza inotify (limite di watch raggiunto) il Lister lo segnala su stderr e continua a funzionare senza auto-refresh.
- Il filtro attivo viene salvato e ripristinato attorno al reload (testo, posizione del cursore e focus): `reload()` passa da `navigate_to()`, che azzera la barra del filtro, quindi senza questo l'auto-refresh cancellava quello che l'utente stava digitando.
- Test: parsing del buffer inotify grezzo (compresa una coda troncata) piu cinque test di integrazione sul watcher vero - creazione file, accorpamento di 200 scritture, eventi di altre directory ignorati, `unwatch`, e sostituzione della directory osservata.
- OPTIMIZE 3.5 - Keybindings modali (vim mode). Nuovo `crates/lister/src/keys.rs`: la tabella dei binding e una funzione pura `resolve(key, ctrl, shift, focus) -> Option<Action>`, testabile senza GTK; `window.rs` si limita a eseguire l'azione risolta. La chiusura inline di ~130 righe che gestiva i tasti e sparita.
- Nuovi tasti: `j`/`k` giu/su, `g`/`G` prima/ultima riga, `l` o `Enter` entra (directory, archivio virtuale o apertura file), `h` o `Backspace` risale al parent, `~` va alla home, `Ins` inverte la selezione della riga sotto il cursore e avanza, `Tab` porta il focus sul Lister con il ruolo opposto (SOURCE <-> DEST).
- Bug preesistente corretto: `c`, `m`, `Del`, `Backspace` e `~` erano elencati nella palette e in `docs/usage.md` ma non erano mai stati collegati a nessun handler. Ora funzionano davvero, insieme a `d` come alias di `Del`.
- `` ` `` apre il terminale e `~` va alla home: prima entrambi i keyval aprivano il terminale, rendendo irraggiungibile la scorciatoia per la home documentata.
- Le lettere singole non rubano piu i caratteri ai campi di testo: il chiamante passa il `Focus` (lista o campo editabile) e in modalita editing restano attivi solo `Esc`, i tasti funzione e le combinazioni `Ctrl`.
- Nuovo cursore di tastiera nel Lister (`Ui.cursor`), sincronizzato con il mouse tramite `selection-changed` e azzerato ad ogni cambio directory. Lo scorrimento passa dall'azione `list.scroll-to-item` di `GtkListBase` perche `ColumnView::scroll_to` richiede GTK 4.12 mentre il progetto e fermo a 4.10.
- Nuovo messaggio `ToHub::PresentLister(ListerId)` gestito dal server per alzare la finestra di un altro Lister.
- OPTIMIZE 3.1 - Theme engine con preset. Nuovo `crates/ipc/src/theme.rs` con `ThemePreset`: Default (light), Amiga Workbench 1.3, Directory Opus 4/5 Classic, Cyberpunk/Tokyo Night, Monochrome Phosphor verde e ambra. Il menu a tendina nella nuova sezione THEME del dialogo Settings riempie tutti i colori in un click; modificando poi un colore a mano il preset memorizzato diventa `custom`.
- `ColorSettings` estesa con `selected_bg`/`selected_fg`: la selezione non e piu inchiodata ai colori del tema GTK. Nuovo blocco `Appearance { preset, file_icons, type_indicators, monospace_list, theme_window }`. Entrambe le struct usano `#[serde(default)]`, quindi un settings.json scritto da una versione precedente continua a caricarsi e i campi mancanti tornano ai default (coperto da test).
- Nuovo `crates/lister/src/glyphs.rs` (puro, senza GTK): glifo Nerd Font per riga scelto per tipo di entry e poi per estensione (rust, python, immagini, audio, video, archivi, shell, ...) e indicatore di tipo stile `ls -F` (`/` directory, `*` eseguibile, `@` symlink, `=` socket, `|` fifo). Entrambi opzionali e spenti di default: i glifi richiedono un Nerd Font installato, altrimenti si vedono quadrati vuoti.
- La CSS del Lister ora dipinge esplicitamente lo sfondo della lista, altrimenti con un preset scuro l'area sotto l'ultima riga restava del colore chiaro del tema GTK. Opzione "Apply theme colours to the whole window" (spenta di default) che estende i colori anche alla cornice ridefinendo `@theme_bg_color`/`@theme_fg_color`.
- Nuovo test che fa analizzare ogni foglio di stile generato dal vero parser CSS di GTK: un selettore sbagliato verrebbe altrimenti scartato in silenzio a runtime. Si salta da solo se non c'e un display.
- OPTIMIZE 2.1 - Copia parallela dei file piccoli. Nuovo modulo `crates/fsops/src/pool.rs` con un `WorkerPool` a dimensione fissa (un thread per core, massimo 8, override via la variabile d'ambiente `OPUSDM_COPY_WORKERS`). I file fino a `SMALL_FILE_MAX` (4 MiB) vengono accodati al pool; i file piu grandi restano sul thread orchestratore per non frammentare l'I/O sequenziale.
- `copy_tree` riscritta attorno a `CopyShared`: contatori atomici (copied/skipped/done_bytes), lista errori sotto `Mutex`, flag sticky `overwrite_all`/`skip_all` e un `conflict_lock` che serializza i prompt, cosi l'utente non vede mai due dialog di conflitto insieme. Directory, symlink e decisioni di conflitto restano sequenziali.
- Il throttling degli eventi `Progress::File` e ora globale al job (50 ms) invece che per singolo file: copiare 100.000 file non genera piu 100.000 eventi IPC forzati.
- `copy_tree_with_workers` interna permette ai test di esercitare sia il percorso sequenziale sia quello con pool; nuovi test su file misti grandi/piccoli, invarianza del risultato al variare dei worker e throttling del progresso.
- Misura su 20.000 file da 8 KiB: 175 ms con 1 worker, 62 ms con 8 (2.8x).

- SVG Icon Toolbar (Directory Opus 5 style): I pulsanti testuali della toolbar e i controlli di navigazione (Home, Parent, Pin) sono stati sostituiti con icone vettoriali SVG ad alta definizione tratte dal set Tabler Icons (salvate in `assets/icons/` ed incorporate a tempo di compilazione senza dipendenze a runtime). Zero emoji.
- Nuovi bottoni icone con tooltip descrittivi: Copy (`copy.svg`), Move (`file-arrow-right.svg`), Delete (`trash.svg`), MakeDir (`folder-plus.svg`), NewFile (`file-plus.svg`), Rename (`edit.svg`), Hidden toggle (`eye.svg` / `eye-off.svg`), Home (`home.svg`), Parent (`arrow-up.svg`), Pin (`pin.svg`).
- Modulo `crates/lister/src/icons.rs`: helper per istanziare `GtkImage` e bottoni GTK4 `flat` con icone SVG scalate nitidamente.

## 2026-09-01

- Lister stat bar: SOURCE/DEST badge is now clickable, cycles SOURCE -> DEST -> NONE (ToHub::SetRole). Pin button (was "Lock" in the button row) moved to the stat bar, left of the badge; same LockRole/UnlockRole semantics. Removed the separate Source/Dest toggle buttons from the button row - role is set by clicking the badge now, not two extra buttons.
- Lister: "Hidden" toggle button in the button row, off by default; filters dotfile rows out of navigate_to(). Ui.show_hidden: Cell<bool>.
- Lister layout redesign: top stat bar (D/F/B counts + SOURCE/DEST/lock), button row, file list (Name/Size/Date/User/Group/Access), bottom navigation (home/parent/path).
- model.rs: Row extended with uid, gid, mode fields; format_user/format_group/format_access functions added.
- window.rs: rebuild_window refactored for new layout; add_column expanded for new column types (User, Group, Access with monospace font).
- update_status() now computes directory/file/byte counts (D/F/B format).
- Role-based stat bar coloring: SOURCE=green, DEST=light red; CSS classes toggle in update_status().
- Hub settings: new settings.rs module (load/save ~/.config/opusdm/settings.json); Settings button opens dialog with SOURCE/DEST color picker (preview box clickable for ColorDialog, hex input).
- IPC: ToLister::Welcome extended with source_color/dest_color fields; new ColorUpdate message to push color changes to connected Listers.
- Server: HubCmd::UpdateColors broadcasts ColorUpdate to all registered Listers when settings saved.
- Lister: receives colors from Welcome + ColorUpdate; applies them dynamically via CSS; update_css_for_colors() generates role-based CSS at runtime.
- Settings model restructured for growth: `opusdm_ipc::Settings { colors: ColorSettings }` is now the single struct used as the wire format (Welcome/SettingsUpdate), the on-disk JSON shape, and the hub's in-memory state - one place to extend when a new setting is added, no more per-field IPC messages. `ColorUpdate` renamed `SettingsUpdate`, carries the whole `Settings`. `HubCmd::UpdateColors` renamed `UpdateSettings`.
- ColorSettings expanded to 4 categories x background+text: source_bg/fg, dest_bg/fg, dir_bg/fg, file_bg/fg. Directory/file rows in the Lister's file list now get their own CSS classes (row-dir/row-file) applied per cell in every column.
- Hub settings dialog: 4 sections (SOURCE/DEST/DIRECTORY/FILE), each with Background + Text color rows; scrollable.

## 2026-08-31

- Task 1: workspace scaffold, opusdm-ipc frame module (length-prefixed JSON frames).

- Task 2: opusdm-ipc protocol enums (ToHub/ToLister), socket path resolution, probe_live_hub.
- Task 3: opusdm-fsops CancelToken, JobSpec/Progress, scan().
- Task 4: opusdm-fsops copy_tree (mode/mtime preserved, conflict + cancel), run_job.
- Task 5: opusdm-fsops run_move, run_delete, make_entry. fsops crate complete.
- Task 6: opusdm-hub crate skeleton, apply_role_event() role machine.
- Task 7: opusdm-hub JobEngine (validate, MAX_CONCURRENT=4, map_progress).
- Task 8: opusdm-hub server.rs - accept loop, owner loop, HubEvent/HubCmd bridge.
- Task 9: opusdm-hub startup - acquire_socket, spawn_lister, install_sigchld; headless main.
- Task 11: opusdm-lister crate, Client::connect + reader thread, env guard in main.
- Task 12: opusdm-lister model.rs (pure): rows, format_size, format_mtime, dir_first_cmp.
- Task 16 (partial): hub lib.rs + integration_copy smoke test (passes), run-x11.sh launches opusdm-hub, QA-checklist.md. GUI tasks 10/13/14/15 pending libgtk-4-dev.
- Task 10: opusdm-hub toolbar.rs GTK window (New Lister, SRC/DST indicators, job rows, Quit); main runs the GTK app. gtk4 pinned to 0.10 + v4_10 (0.11 needs rustc 1.92).
- Task 13: opusdm-lister window.rs - ColumnView (BoxedAnyObject rows), path bar, sort buttons, navigation, focus, status line, inbound pump. actions/dnd stubs.
- Task 14: opusdm-lister actions.rs - Copy/Move/Delete/MakeDir/NewFile/Rename, role toggles, conflict dialog, error dialog. Hub now resolves Copy/Move dest_dir from the DEST Lister path (server.rs); resolve_copy_target is a precondition check.
- Task 15: opusdm-lister dnd.rs - text/uri-list drag source + drop target (Shift=Move). RunJob.dest_dir is now Option: None=button Copy/Move (hub resolves), Some=explicit (DnD/Delete/MakeDir/NewFile).
- Task 16: release build verified; GUI smoke (hub spawns Lister at $HOME, single-instance handoff works); integration_copy passes. v1 feature-complete.
- Post-v1: hub toolbar is now a top-docked X11 panel - undecorated, full monitor width, _NET_WM_WINDOW_TYPE_DOCK + _NET_WM_STRUT_PARTIAL (top=42) so Listers tile below it. New crates/hub/src/dock.rs; deps gdk4-x11, x11rb.
- Fix: openbox was re-centering the DOCK panel. Now sets WM_NORMAL_HINTS user-specified position (0,0) + re-asserts geometry on map. Panel sits flush top-left, full width.
- Feature: Lock button disabled unless the Lister holds SOURCE or DEST.
- Feature: multiple DEST Listers. Role model reworked to a per-Lister map (RoleState.source()/dests()); a locked DEST persists, one unlocked DEST is transient. Button Copy/Move fans out to every DEST (one job per destination). Wire: ToLister RoleChanged/Welcome carry dests: Vec<ListerId>.
- Fix: hub desktop window and panel dock now track a live resolution change (real monitor mode switch, or Xephyr's `-resizeable`) instead of staying pinned to their startup geometry. Both connect to `GdkMonitor::notify::geometry` and re-run their existing make_*_window/pin_geometry setup with the new size. Verified under Xephyr: GDK's X11 backend delivers the geometry change a few seconds after the resize settles, not instantly.
- Fix: the `GdkMonitor::notify::geometry` approach above never fires on a Xephyr host-window resize - measured: the root window grows, but GDK keeps reporting the old monitor size indefinitely, because RandR is still a step behind at the instant the resize event arrives and GDK only ever re-reads it on such an event. Replaced by `opusdm_ui::watch_screen_size`, a dedicated x11rb connection that selects `StructureNotify` on the root window and forwards every root `ConfigureNotify` (the size a screen change carries, no extension needed) to the GTK main loop; RandR is consulted only to pick the primary monitor on a multi-head screen. Hub desktop and panel dock now re-apply their geometry from that.
- Fix: hub desktop and panel dock are `resizable(true)` on the GTK side. A non-resizable GTK window keeps asking the WM for its natural size, which undid the X-level geometry and made the windows unable to shrink when the screen got smaller. They stay fixed for the user through the min = max `WM_NORMAL_HINTS` the two `apply()` helpers already install.
- Fix: `desktop::pin_geometry` also sends `_NET_MOVERESIZE_WINDOW`. Once the WM has reparented the window, the position in a plain `ConfigureWindow` is relative to the frame, so openbox kept parking the desktop at the work area origin (below the panel strut) instead of at 0,0.
- Feature: file operations progress moved out of the Lister status line into a dedicated hub-owned window (crates/hub/src/progress_window.rs), one block per running job (filename + per-file progress bar + total n/tot files bar, separators between concurrent jobs), shown/hidden automatically. fsops::Progress::File now also carries file_bytes_done/file_bytes_total (per current file, not just job-wide totals) so the per-file bar reflects the actual file being copied; ToLister::JobProgress and HubEvent::JobUpdate carry the same fields through to the hub.

## 2026-09-12

- Fix: Breeze Dark icon PNGs (`crates/iconview/assets/breeze-dark/`) were 32x32 flat-color placeholder swatches, not real icon art, showing as plain blue/yellow squares in the Name column. Re-rendered from the installed `kf6-breeze-icon-theme` SVGs via GdkPixbuf at 256px (ImageMagick's built-in SVG renderer ignores the CSS classes Breeze uses for its accent colours, hence the earlier placeholder look if rendered that way).
- Feature: custom themes. `IconSetId` drops the compiled-in `BreezeDark` variant and shrinks to `{None, Custom(String)}` - no icon set ships in the binary anymore. A theme is a folder under `~/.config/opusdm/themes/<name>/` (`manifest.json` identity, `theme.json` colours/appearance/behavior/desktop-metrics/background, `icons/`, `backgrounds/`, `preview.png`), installed only by importing a `.zip`/`.tar.gz`/`.tar.bz2` archive through `opusdm-theme`'s new "Import theme..." button (zip-slip/tar-slip guarded, all-or-nothing, overwrite-on-reimport). Applying a `Custom` theme is a one-shot partial-override merge over the user's current colours/appearance/behavior/desktop metrics/background - fields the theme omits keep their current value. New `opusdm-ipc::themes` module (discovery/parsing), `crates/theme-config/src/{apply,importer}.rs` (merge logic, archive extraction). Breeze Dark itself moved to `themes/breeze-dark/` as the first importable package, built into `breeze-dark.zip` via `cargo run -p xtask -- package-themes`. A pre-migration `settings.json`'s literal `"BreezeDark"` tag deserializes to `None` rather than failing the whole `Settings` parse. No new IPC message types; a custom theme's `appearance`/`behavior` overrides are persisted straight into `settings.json` since no message exists to push them to a *running* hub (effective on its next restart).

## 2026-09-14

- Perf (OPTIMIZE.md): hub owner loop no longer blocks on socket writes or settings persistence. Each `Conn` now writes through a per-connection channel to its own writer thread (`server::writer_loop`) instead of a blocking `send()` on the owner thread, so one non-reading Lister can no longer stall connection lifecycle, role changes, or job progress for everyone else; a separate socket clone (`Conn::shutdown`) is kept only to tear the connection down. `settings.json`/`desktop.json` writes moved to a dedicated `opusdm-persist` thread (`PersistMsg`) fed from the owner loop, decoupling slow filesystem I/O from message handling. Tests that asserted the settings/desktop file existed immediately after the "changed" event now poll (`wait_for_file`) since persistence is async.

## 2026-09-17

- Feature: multi-monitor support for the panel bar and the desktop root window. New `opusdm_ui::list_monitors`/`watch_monitors` (`crates/ui/src/screen.rs`) enumerate distinct monitor rects from `GdkDisplay::monitors()`, deduping identical rects so a mirrored output (same geometry reported twice) collapses to one entry instead of stacking duplicate windows. `opusdm-panel::attach` now builds one full bar per distinct rect (menu/pager/tasklist/sysmon/clock duplicated on each, EWMH state shared and fanned out to all); `_NET_WM_STRUT_PARTIAL`'s `top_start_x`/`top_end_x` are now offset by the monitor's own `x`, not always root-relative from 0. `opusdm-hub`'s desktop window is likewise one per distinct rect, index 0 treated as primary: only the primary gets the interactive icon field, drop target and inotify watch, the rest are background-only (no placeholder label either, so "OpusDM" doesn't repeat across monitors). `crates/hub/src/desktop.rs` and `crates/panel/src/window.rs` window-hint helpers (`make_*_window`/`pin_geometry`/`apply`) gained `(x, y)` origin parameters instead of always pinning to `0,0`. Live layout changes (resolution switch, hotplug, mirror toggled at runtime) go through `reconcile_desktop_windows`: same monitor count just re-pins the existing windows in place (keeps the primary's icon field/watch alive), a count change tears down and rebuilds the whole set.

## 2026-09-19

- Fix: dropped the "OpusDM" placeholder label shown centered on the primary desktop window's background when no desktop directory resolves. `build_desktop_area` no longer takes a `show_placeholder` flag; the `.opusdm-desktop-label` CSS rule and its test assertion are gone too.

## 2026-09-27

- Feature: the desktop window now offers the core Lister file operations from its right-click menus (no toolbar exists there). Right-clicking a desktop icon shows a per-item menu - Open, Cut, Copy, Rename..., Delete... - built in `crates/hub/src/toolbar.rs::fill_icon_menu`; right-clicking empty desktop space adds Paste, New Folder..., New File... and Open Terminal Here to the existing background menu. New `crates/hub/src/desktop_ops.rs` holds the action logic (clipboard cut/copy/paste via the system clipboard, mirroring `lister::clip`; rename/delete/mkdir/newfile via `HubCmd::RunJob`; terminal launch via `$TERMINAL` or a common fallback list with `current_dir` set). `attach_desktop_menu` now dispatches through `IconField::connect_context_menu` (idx >= 0 = icon menu, idx < 0 = background menu) instead of a bare overlay-level click, using a new `IconField::translate_from_canvas` (`crates/iconview/src/field.rs`) to map its Fixed-space click coordinates into the overlay's coordinate space for positioning the hand-rolled menu box (the desktop window can't grab a `Popover`).
- Verified (code review, not newly added): dragging files from a Lister onto the desktop already worked end-to-end - `IconField`'s drag source (Grid mode, used by Lister) and the desktop's `connect_drop_uris` -> `HubCmd::RunJob` (wired in `wire_primary_icon_field`) were already in place, sharing the same `text/uri-list` codec as Lister-to-Lister drops. No changes made there.
- Known gap (pre-existing, not addressed here): a hub-originated job's overwrite conflicts (`Progress::Conflict`) are never routed anywhere, since `GTK_REQUESTER` is not a real Lister connection - a Copy/Move/Paste run from the desktop that hits an existing-file conflict currently has no prompt and will stall. Same limitation already existed for drag-drop-onto-desktop before this change.

## 2026-09-28

- Feature (F-01, Function Editor): the first piece of OpusDM's Function system - a named, ordered list of steps (an internal command or an external/bash command with `{f}`/`{F}`/`{o}`/`{O}`/`{df}`/`{dF}`/`{do}`/`{dO}` argument placeholders) editable in a GTK4 window, test-runnable for real, saved to `~/.config/opusdm/functions.json`, and runnable again from the Command Palette. New `opusdm_ipc::function` module: `Function`/`FnStep`/`InternalCmd` data model, per-command parameter schema (`schema::params_for`), `validate()`, shell-quoted placeholder expansion (`expand_text`), and `functions.json` persistence. `InternalCmd` covers the six operations the hub's job engine already performs: Copy, Move, Delete, MakeDir, NewFile, Rename.
- IPC: new `ToHub::RunFunction`/`SelectionReply` and `ToLister::QuerySelection`/`FunctionOutput`/`FunctionStepDone`/`FunctionFailed`/`FunctionDone` messages.
- Hub: `crates/hub/src/functions.rs` executes a Function's steps on their own thread once SOURCE and (if any) DEST selection have been collected via a `QuerySelection`/`SelectionReply` round trip modeled on the existing `ConflictPrompt`/`ConflictReply` pending-request pattern (`server.rs`'s new `PendingFunctionRun` bookkeeping). Internal steps reuse `opusdm_fsops::run_job` (Copy/Move/Delete) or a direct `std::fs::rename`/`create_dir`/`File::create` (Rename/MakeDir/NewFile); external steps spawn a subprocess with piped stdout/stderr, streamed back line by line. A destination conflict during a Function-driven Copy/Move/Delete is always auto-skipped (no conflict-prompt UI exists for Function steps yet) rather than left to deadlock the executor thread waiting on a reply nobody would send.
- Lister: new `crates/lister/src/function_editor.rs` - a "Manage Functions" window (list, New/Edit/Delete) and a per-function editor (reorderable step list mirroring `desktop-config`'s Menu tab editor, a live-validated required-parameter form per internal step, a Test button that really runs the function-as-edited and streams output into a log panel, a Save button disabled while any required parameter is unfilled). `CommandId`/`Action` dropped their `Copy` derive (a `FunctionId` payload isn't `Copy`) to add `CommandId::ManageFunctions`/`RunFunction(FunctionId)`; the Command Palette's entry list is now built fresh on every open (`get_commands() -> Vec<CommandItem>`, `CommandItem::title`/`category` now `Cow<'static, str>`) with one dynamic "Function" entry per saved function.
- Design/plan: `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-09-28-function-editor-design.md`, `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-09-28-function-editor.md`.
- Docs: added `TODO.md`, a checklist of the F-01..F-50 roadmap from `FEATURES.md` (F-01 core marked done, follow-ups listed).
- Docs: added the design spec for F-03 Hotkeys, F-04 User menu and F-05 Script events (`docs/superpowers/specs/2026-09-29-hotkeys-usermenu-scripts-design.md`).
- Docs: added the implementation plan for F-03/F-04/F-05 (`docs/superpowers/plans/2026-09-29-hotkeys-usermenu-scripts.md`).
- Feature (F-03/F-04/F-05, step 1): new `opusdm_ipc::bindings` module - hotkey/menu/script-event data model, `Ctrl+Alt+u` key parser, per-section validators, tolerant `sanitize`, `prune`, and persistence to `hotkeys.json`, `usermenu.json`, `scripts.json`.
- Feature (F-03/F-04/F-05, step 2): `ToHub::SetBindings` / `ToLister::BindingsUpdate` / `Welcome.bindings`. The hub loads, sanitizes, persists and broadcasts `Bindings` (`ServerOptions` keeps tests off the real config); listers cache them in `HubConnection.bindings`.
- Feature (F-03/F-04/F-05, step 3): `ToHub::RunFunctionById` and hub-originated ("headless") Function runs (`HubCmd::RunFunctionHeadless`, requester sentinel `u64::MAX`, SOURCE = current SOURCE Lister or an empty selection in $HOME). `selection_waiters` is now a per-Lister FIFO so two runs waiting on one Lister both complete.
- Feature (F-03, step 4): local user hotkeys - the Lister keeps a `HotkeyMap` built from `Bindings`, consults it before `keys::resolve` (the user wins over built-ins), and never fires a bare letter while a text field has focus.
- Feature (F-03, step 5): "Edit Hotkeys..." editor (key capture, function drop-down, Global flag, inline errors, Save gated by `validate_hotkeys`) and "Find Key..." requester in the Command Palette.
- Feature (F-03, step 6): system-global hotkeys - the hub grabs them with `XGrabKey` on a lazily created X11 connection (all NumLock/CapsLock variants), runs the bound function headless, logs keys another client already owns, and stays inert without X11 (Wayland unsupported).
- Feature (F-04, step 7): user menu - a "Menu" drop-down in the lister button row (popup built lazily from the cached bindings, entries run functions, "Edit User Menu..." always present) and a tree editor with add/rename/move/remove; tree operations live in `opusdm_ipc::bindings`.
- Feature (F-05, step 8): script events - the hub emits Startup, Shutdown (waits up to 10 s for the hooks), ListerOpened, ListerClosed and the three desktop double clicks, runs the bound functions headless, throttles loops with an in-flight guard plus a 2 s cooldown, and "Edit Script Events..." edits the bindings.
- Docs: documented hotkeys, the user menu and script events in `docs/usage.md` (sections 3.13 to 3.15) and ticked F-03, F-04, F-05 and item 2 of the suggested order in `TODO.md`.
- Fix: a binding file that fails to parse (e.g. an unknown event name) is copied to `<name>.bak` before the empty section can be saved over it.
- Fix: quitting the hub now waits (capped at the Shutdown hook timeout) for the Shutdown hooks through `HubCmd::ShutdownAndWait`, so exiting the process no longer kills a running hook.

## 2026-09-29

- Docs: added the design spec for the job engine base (F-15 recursive filter, F-07 Copy/Move variants and flags), sub-project A of item 3 of the suggested order (`docs/superpowers/specs/2026-09-29-job-engine-base-design.md`).
- Feature (F-15, F-07, sub-project A): job options and copy variants. `opusdm_ipc::JobOptions` (`update`, `newer`, `move_when_same`, `quiet`, `include_glob`) travels in `ToHub::RunJob` (`#[serde(default)]`, old payloads still parse); `Op` gains `CopyAs`, `MoveAs`, `Duplicate`. New `opusdm_ipc::glob` (case-insensitive `*`/`?` matcher, `;`/`,` separated `GlobSet`, `*` expansion for new names).
- fsops: `scan_with` filters files by glob (directories always walked, empty ones dropped) and renames the top-level entry; the copy pass applies `update`/`newer` before any conflict prompt; a filtered or flagged Move goes through the copy engine and deletes only the files it really copied, then the directories that became empty; `Duplicate` picks `name.copy`, `name.copy2`, ...; `move_when_same` turns a same-device Copy into a Move; filtered Delete removes only matching files. Bad name lists, duplicate names and empty globs are rejected before any file is touched.
- Hub/Function Editor: the internal Copy/Move/Delete commands take the flags and a filter, `CopyAs`, `MoveAs`, `Duplicate` are new internal commands (checkbox parameters are rendered as checkboxes). Hub refreshes source directories after every job, since Duplicate adds to them and Copy may run as a Move.
- Lister: palette entries "Copy As...", "Move As..." (naming dialog with live preview), "Duplicate Selected Items", "Copy (newer only)". New `Behavior.recursive_filter` setting (Theme Config checkbox): all Copy/Move/Delete submissions go through `job_submit::submit_job`, which asks for a file pattern first when a directory is involved.
