- Docs: `docs/usage.md` section 3.16, plan `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-09-29-job-engine-base.md`; ticked F-07 and F-15 in `TODO.md`.
- Feature (F-09, F-10, sub-project B): `Op::Chmod { mode, recurse }` and `Op::Touch { unix_secs, recurse }`, run by the new `opusdm_fsops::attrs` walker (children before their directory, symlinks never followed, `include_glob` limits it to matching files and leaves directories alone). Chmod gives directories `x` wherever `r` is set and skips symlinks; Touch stamps a symlink itself. New `opusdm_ipc::timefmt` (local `YYYY-MM-DD HH:MM:SS` via libc). Palette entries "Change Permissions..." (rwx grid synced with an octal field, recursive toggle) and "Set Timestamp..." (Now button, apply to contents); both go through `job_submit::submit_job`, so the recursive filter prompt applies when they recurse. Docs: `docs/usage.md` 3.17, design `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-09-29-file-ops-variants-design.md`.
- Feature (F-12, F-13, sub-project C): `Op::Link { symbolic, new_names }`, `Op::Join { target }`, `Op::Split { chunk_bytes }`. `opusdm_fsops::links` creates symbolic (absolute target) or hard links, never overwrites, refuses hard links to directories and keeps going after a failed entry. `opusdm_fsops::joinsplit` streams Join (checks every source and the target first, removes the partial target on error or cancel) and Split (`name.001`..., width grows past 999 parts, refuses when a part exists, removes written parts on error or cancel). Palette entries "Make Link...", "Join Files...", "Split File..." (`link_dialogs.rs`, size parser and default join name unit tested). Docs: `docs/usage.md` 3.18.
- Feature (F-08, F-31, sub-project D): `opusdm_fsops::checkfit` (`needed_bytes` recursive without following symlinks, `free_bytes` via `statvfs` `f_bavail * f_frsize`, `compute` with the capped percentage). New `ToHub::CheckFit` / `ToLister::CheckFitResult` / `CheckFitFailed`: the hub sizes on its own thread and answers the requester; the Lister shows a requester ("Check Fit" palette entry). Reselect: `Ui::last_selection` is filled when a job is submitted (`job_submit::submit_job`) and on Deselect All / Esc; palette "Reselect Previous Selection" and `Ctrl+Shift+A` (Ctrl+A still selects all) restore the names still listed. New hub integration tests (ignored like the others): CheckFit answer and a Duplicate job with options. Note: `copy_fans_out_to_all_locked_dests` (ignored test) already fails on the commit before this work, unrelated. Docs: `docs/usage.md` 3.19. Item 3 of the suggested order is complete.

- Feature (F-29): type-to-find in the Lister (`crates/lister/src/typeahead.rs`). A letter, digit, `.` or `_` over the file list opens a buffer; the cursor jumps to the first name with that prefix (case ignored), the status line shows `Find: <text>`, `Backspace` edits, `Esc` closes, 1.5 s idle closes. Always on, no setting.
- Removed the vim-style single-letter bindings (`j k g G l h c m d`) and `keys::Motion`/`Action::Move`, since they clash with type-to-find. Copy, Move and Delete moved to `F5`, `F6`, `F8` (`Del` unchanged); `Enter`, `Backspace`, `~`, `Ins`, `Tab` are unchanged. Palette shortcut labels and the hotkey editor's KeyFinder follow.
- Feature (F-32): `Behavior.status_format` in `settings.json` (default `D: [%D/%d]  F: [%F/%f]  B: [%B/%b]`) drives the status bar through the pure `status_format::format_status`. Codes: `%d %f %b` totals, `%D %F %B` selection, `%s` selected entries, `%p` path, `%%`. Editable in the BEHAVIOUR section of Settings; an empty value means the default.
- Feature (F-22, F-23): Lister formats. New `opusdm_ipc::format` (`ListerFormat`, `FormatStore`, resolve by exact entry / inheriting ancestor / default / built-in, `sanitized`, `formats.json` load with `.bak` on parse errors) and `glob::NameFilter` (show/hide lists with `!` exclusions, files only). The hub owns and persists `formats.json` (`ToHub::SetFormats`, `ToLister::FormatsUpdate`, `Welcome.formats`). The Lister builds its six columns once and applies the effective format on every navigation (`format_apply.rs`): column visibility and order, sort column and direction, dir/file separation (`dir_first_cmp` takes `Separate`), row filter, free-space gauge (`gauge.rs`, `statvfs`). "Lister Format..." dialog and "Set Show/Hide Pattern..." in the palette (`format_editor.rs`).
- Feature (F-24): directory cache, history and Cache List. New `dircache.rs` (LRU of raw listings per Lister, `same_listing` order-insensitive comparison) and `history.rs` (browser style stack, 50 entries). Navigating to a cached directory shows it at once and always re-reads the disk on a thread, replacing the view only when the listing changed (selection kept by name); reload, auto-refresh and job refreshes bypass the cache; archives are never cached. Back / forward / history-menu widgets beside the path bar, `Alt+Left` / `Alt+Right`, palette "History Back/Forward". Protocol: `ToHub::{CacheReport, QueryCaches, FreeAllCaches, JumpTo}`, `ToLister::{CacheList, FreeCaches, GoTo}`, `CacheEntry`; the hub keeps each Lister's reported paths per connection. "Cache List..." dialog (double click raises the owning Lister, "Open here", "Free all caches") and "Free Caches" in the palette. `Behavior.cache_size` (default 20, clamped 0..=200) with a spin button in the BEHAVIOUR section of Settings.
- Feature (F-21): "Advanced Select..." requester. New pure `select_criteria.rs` (`Criteria`, `Fields`, `KindFilter`, `parse_when`): name pattern lists with `!` exclusions (reusing `NameFilter`), type, size range (Split's size syntax) and modified-time range (absolute local time or relative `30m`/`12h`/`7d`/`2w`), all combined with AND; a size limit never matches a directory; errors name the field. `advanced_select.rs` is the dialog with Select and Deselect over the listed rows. Palette entry in the Selection category.
- Feature (F-16, F-17): Viewer window and image slideshow. New pure modules `ansi.rs` (CP437 table, SGR colours, bold brightening, cursor-forward, SUB cut, `Span`/`AnsiDoc`), `viewer_search.rs` (`find_matches` with character offsets, wrapping `step`), `slideshow.rs` (`Marks`), `viewer_mode.rs` (`Mode`, `Resolved`, SmartRead `resolve`, `viewer_files`, `image_files`) and `viewer_load.rs` (capped loading into `ViewData`: text/ANSI 16 MiB, hex 1 MiB, a "showing the first N" notice). `preview.rs` shares `IMAGE_EXTS` and `kind_of`. `viewer.rs` is the window: header `i/n name`, text/ANSI/hex/image content, `Ctrl+F` search bar with highlight and counter, `n`/`p` paging, `t a x s` mode keys; Show keeps the images of the selection, `Delete`/`m` marks, and closing asks before deleting the marked files through the job engine. Palette: Read (SmartRead), Read as Text / ANSI / Hex, Show (image slideshow). Quick Look is unchanged. Configurable read caps are recorded in `NEXT-PHASE.md`.
- Feature (F-19, F-20): Find Files and Search in Files. New pure `find_search.rs`: `FindQuery`, `scan_text` (first hit with 1-based line, trimmed 120-character snippet, occurrence count; NUL in the first 8 KiB means binary; lossy UTF-8), `run_search` (walkdir without following links, hidden names and their subtrees skipped unless asked, cancel flag checked per entry, `Scanned` progress every 200 entries, `MAX_RESULTS` 5000, `SEARCH_FILE_MAX` 16 MiB, unreadable entries counted as skipped), plus `result_label` and `status_text`. `find_dialog.rs` is the single Find dialog (Name, Contains, Folder, Case sensitive, Include hidden) that streams `SearchEvent`s from a worker thread over an `async_channel`; new search, Stop and Close cancel the running one and a generation counter drops late events. "Show in folder" (`Ui::show_in_folder`) and "View" (Viewer on the first match through `ViewerOptions::initial_search`). Palette: "Find Files...", "Search in Files...". The search limits are recorded in `NEXT-PHASE.md`.

## 2026-10-01

- Fix: type-to-find swallows Backspace while active, including the grace period after the buffer was emptied, so extra Backspaces no longer walk up the folder tree.
- Fix: `~` (Home) also accepts the dead tilde keyval and any key whose character is `~`, for layouts such as the Italian one where it is typed with AltGr.
- Fix: the keypad `*` (`KP_Multiply`) inverts the selection like the main `*`.
- Change: new folder moved from `F7` to `Ctrl+N` and new file from `Shift+F7` to `Ctrl+Shift+N` (only over the file list, not inside text fields). Palette labels and docs follow.
- Change: `F8` no longer deletes; only `Del` does. Palette label and docs follow.
- Fix: the rename dialog (F2 and the toolbar button) preselects only the name, leaving the extension out of the selection (`actions::rename_selection`, new `opusdm_ui::requester_entry_selecting`). Directories and dotfiles are selected whole.
- Fix: Ctrl+H (and the palette entry) now toggles the toolbar eye button, so its state and icon follow (`Ui::hidden_btn`).
- Fix: palette "Set Role: DEST/NONE" now works. Closing the palette gave the focus back to the Lister, and the hub turned the focused window into SOURCE right after the request; the request now waits until the window is active (`palette::request_role`).
- Feature: right click on the toolbar Copy / Move buttons opens Copy As... / Move As... (tooltips say so).
- Feature: Copy As... / Move As... on several items open the batch renamer rules dialog (find/replace, prefix, suffix, case, numbering, live preview) and submit the previewed names; `batch_rename::open_rules_dialog` is shared with Batch Rename. A single item keeps the pattern dialog.
- Fix: `run-x11.sh` starts Xephyr with `-no-host-grab`, so it no longer captures the mouse and keyboard inside its window.
- Feature (F-06): file types. New `opusdm_ipc::filetypes` (`FileType`, `MatchRule` with Glob, ContentBytes, ContentText, SizeRange, DateRange, And, Or; `FileTypes::find`, `derive_rule` for the Filetype Creator, hex helpers, `filetypes.json` load with `.bak` on parse errors and save). The Lister reads the file on demand: a double click on a file runs the matching type's function (selecting that file first) before falling back to the default application; right click shows its "Open With" entries. New `filetype_form.rs` (pure conditions <-> rule form) and `filetypes_ui.rs` (editor with ordered list, conditions, actions) plus palette entries "Edit File Types..." and "Filetype Creator (from selected file)...". The `drop` action is stored but not wired. Docs: `docs/usage.md` 3.10h.
- Feature (F-01 follow-up): conflict prompts for Function steps. A Copy/Move step that meets an existing destination now sends `FnEvent::Conflict` to the hub, which forwards a `ToLister::ConflictPrompt` to the Lister that started the run and relays its `ConflictReply` to the job (previously every conflict was skipped). Runs with no Lister to ask (script events, Shutdown hooks) or whose Lister is gone still skip; a Lister that closes while a prompt is open releases the waiting job (`fn_conflict_owners`). Tests: relayed Skip / Overwrite / Cancel, a dropped reply does not hang, hub round trip and the headless skip. Docs: `docs/usage.md` 3.10h.
- Feature (F-39): FTP / FTPS / SFTP. New crate `opusdm-ftp` (`suppaftp` with native-tls, `ssh2`): `RemoteUrl`, `RemoteFs` trait with `SftpFs` and `FtpFs`, `AddressBook` (`ftp.json`), `KnownHosts` (`known_hosts.json`, SHA-256 fingerprints, unknown / changed host detection) and `download` (recursive, refuses names with `/`, `..` or NUL, link-loop depth cap, no partial files). Tests: unit tests, an acceptance test against a real `sshd` the test starts on loopback (skips when `sshd` is missing) and an in-process FTP server.
- Lister: remote paths in the path bar and address book dialog (`remote.rs` pure helpers, `remote_ui.rs` sessions, host key approval, password prompt, download). Listing runs on a worker thread, remote folders are not cached or watched, Copy from a remote folder downloads to a scratch folder and submits a normal job, double click downloads and opens the file. Palette: "FTP / SFTP Address Book...", "FTP Quit". The hub refuses any job whose source or destination is a remote path. Docs: `docs/usage.md` 3.10i.
- Feature (F-14): Encrypt / Decrypt. New `opusdm_fsops::crypt` (XChaCha20-Poly1305 STREAM in 64 KiB chunks, Argon2id key per file, authenticated header, `.part` file renamed on success, never overwrites, folders keep their tree, cancel supported) and `Op::Encrypt` / `Op::Decrypt` carrying `opusdm_ipc::Secret` (plain on the wire, `Debug` shows `<hidden>`). The hub only requires a non-empty selection for both. Lister: `crypt_dialog.rs` password requester (repeat field when encrypting) and palette entries "Encrypt..." / "Decrypt...". Tests: round trips around chunk boundaries, wrong password, tampering, truncation, foreign files, existing targets, cancel, job engine dispatch. Docs: `docs/usage.md` 3.10j.
- Feature (F-14): the file context menu has "Encrypt..." and, on `.enc` files, "Decrypt...". They act on the whole selection when the clicked file is part of it, otherwise on the clicked file only (`crypt_dialog::targets_for`, unit tested). `open_crypt` now takes the paths to work on.
- Feature (F-39 follow-up): remote write operations. `RemoteFs` gains `write_file`, `mkdir`, `remove_file`, `remove_dir` (SFTP and FTP/FTPS); `opusdm_ftp::upload`, `remove_tree`, `resolve_items`, `existing_remote`. Tests against the real `sshd` and the in-process FTP server (upload tree, replace, recursive delete) plus in-memory fakes.
- Hub: a job whose source or destination is remote is handed back to the requester as `ToLister::RemoteTransfer { op, srcs, dest_dir }` with the DEST already resolved (replacing the refusal; `validate_op` still refuses as a safety net). New `ToHub::Invalidate(path)` makes every Lister reload that folder.
- Lister: `remote_xfer.rs` plans Copy / Move / Delete / New Folder / New File as Download, Upload, Relay, Remove or Create (pure, tested), connects, asks Overwrite / Skip existing / Cancel, runs the transfer on a worker thread behind a progress window with Cancel, deletes Move sources only after success, then refreshes the affected folders.

- Feature (F-40): `Confirm` internal command (parameter `message`, with placeholders). The executor emits `FnEvent::Confirm`, the hub asks the starting Lister with `ToLister::ConfirmPrompt` and relays `ToHub::ConfirmReply`; a no, a closed Lister or a run with nobody to ask fails the step, so the function stops. Tests: executor (yes, no, no answer, empty question, placeholder expansion) and a hub round trip. Docs: `docs/usage.md` 3.10k.
- Feature (F-41): busy indicator. `Ui::start_run` is now the single way a Lister hands a function to the hub (palette, hotkeys, user menu, filetype actions, context menu, Function Editor Test); it counts the run in the pure `busy::Busy` and shows a spinner and "Running ..." text until `FunctionDone` / `FunctionFailed` arrives. Docs: `docs/usage.md` 3.10l.
- Feature (F-42): internal command line. New pure `opusdm_ipc::cli` (`tokenize`, `find_command` with variant name, label and aliases, `parse` into `CliCommand::{Shell, Internal, Function}`, positional / `key=value` / checkbox arguments checked against the parameter schema, required parameters validated) and `InternalCmd::ALL`. Lister: `cli_dialog.rs` prompt and palette entry "Command Line...". Docs: `docs/usage.md` 3.10m.
- Feature (F-43): `ChangeDir` options `new`, `container` and `mode`. `FnEvent::ChangeDir` carries `select`, `mode` and `new_lister`; the hub spawns a Lister for `new` and otherwise sends the new `ToLister::ScanDir { path, select, mode }`, which the Lister answers by setting the view toggle, navigating and selecting the entry. Tests: container with a file, a directory and no flag; mode check; new flag. Docs: `docs/usage.md` 3.10n.
- Test fixes: the `sshd` helper waits for the SSH banner before the SFTP tests start, and the hub's remote bounce test waits until the DEST path is recorded (two connections race otherwise).
- Feature (F-44): "Go to Root", "Open Parent in NEW Lister" and "Open Root in NEW Lister" (palette, Navigation). New pure `navpath.rs` (`parent`, `root` for local, archive and remote folders, unit tested); the parent button and `ParentDir` use it, which also fixes Backspace inside archives. Docs: `docs/usage.md` 3.10o.
- Feature (F-45): GetSizes FORCE. New `sizecache.rs` (`SizeCache::split` separates known sizes from folders to calculate, force drops the targets' entries; unit tested); `tools::calculate_dir_sizes_async(ui, force)` reuses remembered sizes, shows them on later listings, and the palette has "Calculate Directory Sizes (force rescan)". The cache is cleared after jobs and by Free Caches. Docs: `docs/usage.md` 3.10p.
- Feature (F-46): `Run` internal command (`command`, `wait`, `window`). `wait` alone reuses the External step; otherwise the program is started, reaped by a thread, and with `window` its output streams as `FnEvent::Window` (`WindowMsg::{Open, Line, Closed}`). The hub keeps `output_windows` by `(run, step)` so the output reaches the starting Lister after the function ended (`ToLister::OutputOpen / OutputLine / OutputEnd`); `output_window.rs` shows it. Extracted `expand_argv`, shared with External. Tests: async start and reaping, wait with good and bad exits, window stream and exit code, window with wait, code expansion, bad commands, and a hub test that output arrives after `FunctionDone`. Docs: `docs/usage.md` 3.10q.
- Feature (F-48): bookmarks as the Assign equivalent. New `opusdm_ipc::bookmarks` (`Bookmarks::resolve` for `name:` and `name:sub/dir` without escaping the bookmark or clashing with `sftp://`, name and folder checks, `bookmarks.json` with `.bak` on parse errors; unit tested). Lister: `bookmarks_ui.rs` (manager, add current folder, path bar lookup) and palette entries "Bookmarks...", "Add Bookmark for This Folder...". Docs: `docs/usage.md` 3.10r.
- Feature (F-49): "View Font..." previewer (`font_viewer.rs`): searchable list of the installed families (Pango font map), size, Bold / Italic and an editable sample, styled through a CSS provider that is removed when the window closes. Pure helpers `filter_families`, `description` and `sample_css` (family escaping) are unit tested. Docs: `docs/usage.md` 3.10s.
- Fix: `save_json` is atomic (sibling temp file, `fsync`, `rename`), so a crash or a full disk cannot truncate a config file. Tests: replace leaves no temp file, a failed save keeps the old file.
- Fix: `load_json` copies a malformed file to `<name>.bak` (an existing backup is kept) before returning the defaults, so settings, Functions and left-out icons are no longer destroyed by the next save.
- Perf: icon textures are decoded once per `(theme, icon file)` (`opusdm_iconview::icon_set::icon_texture_for`, thread-local cache) and shared by the list Name column and the icon view; scrolling no longer decodes PNG/SVG on every row bind.
- Fix: `extract_single_file_temp` returns the real extracted path for nested entries, checks every exit status (a failed `unzip -p` no longer shows an empty file), fails on a missing entry and supports `.7z` instead of falling to `tar`; unknown formats are refused with `Unsupported`. `extract_items_to_dir` does the same and handles `.7z`.
- Fix: archive entry names are never read as options or wildcards (`--` and `--no-wildcards` for `tar`, `-spd` and `--` for `7z`, escaped pattern for `unzip`); names with `..` are refused. Tests build real zip / tar / 7z archives with names such as `-dash.txt` and `st[ar]*.txt`.
- Fix: archive previews go to a unique private folder (`tempfile`, mode 0700) instead of the shared `/tmp/opusdm-preview`, and the Lister removes them on exit (`archive::cleanup_previews`).
- Fix: Copy / Move out of an archive extracts on a worker thread (`actions::transfer_from_archive`, `glib::spawn_future_local`), so the Lister no longer freezes on big archives.
- Fix: extraction goes to a unique `tempfile::TempDir` per operation instead of the shared `opusdm-extract-<pid>`; it is deleted when the jobs it feeds are done (`archive::keep_scratch` / `scratch_job_done`, one reply per DEST Lister) and anything left is removed at exit (`archive::cleanup_temp_dirs`). A failed preview extraction removes its folder at once. A failure to create the folder is shown in the status line.
- Fix: Move from an archive no longer reports a Move that left the archive untouched: it runs as a Copy and the status line says why.
- Fix: archive extraction no longer ignores setup errors (the `flash` message carries the real cause). The remote paths already propagated theirs (`remote::new_download_dir` with `?`), so nothing changed there.
- Refactor: `wire_transfer` handles the archive case first and then resolves the selection and submits once for local and remote folders (they were identical).
- Perf: changing the Size Format rebinds the rows (`touch_rows`, selection restored by name) instead of re-reading the directory.
- Fix: the Modified column computes the timezone offset per timestamp (`local_tz_offset_secs_at`) at bind time, so it follows DST changes and a file from another season keeps the offset that was valid on its date. Test with `TZ=Europe/Rome`.
- Refactor: `format_user` and `format_group` share one `cached_name` helper (tested: one resolve per id).
- Perf: `save_functions` serializes the slice directly (`save_json` accepts unsized values), no copy of the list.
- Refactor: the "read, parse, back up to `.bak`, log" sequence lives only in `settings::load_json` / `settings::read_json`; bookmarks, formats, file types, bindings, button banks and groups use them. One policy everywhere: an existing backup is kept. Groups now also back up a malformed file.
- Refactor: the pure "rows -> visible rows + totals" step of `apply_navigation` is `model::visible_rows` (hidden / `.opusdm` / name filter, remembered directory sizes, totals), unit tested without GTK.
- Refactor: a command is described once. `keys.rs` declares `CommandId` through the `commands!` macro (title, category, shortcut next to the variant, `CommandId::meta()`, `CommandId::BUILTIN`); the palette table is built from it, so a variant can no longer be missing from the palette (it happened with `SizeFormat`). The palette order is the order in the macro.
- Refactor: `palette::execute_command` is a table of one line arms (still exhaustive); the longer bodies moved to `commands.rs` grouped by category, with the Copy / Move / Copy newer and the Select / Deselect by pattern and New folder / New file pairs sharing one function each.
- Refactor: `hub/src/toolbar.rs` (1,670 lines) is split into `toolbar.rs` (app, `Widgets`, event pump, quit), `desktop_windows.rs` (monitor windows, wallpaper CSS, desktop watch, hide / reveal), `desktop_menu.rs` (right-click menus, double click events, About) and `groups_ui.rs` (groups and banks). Code moved unchanged; only visibility widened to `pub(crate)`.
- Refactor: `hub/src/server.rs` loses the Function-run state machine (`server/function_run.rs`: `PendingFunctionRun` and the `impl Hub` methods start / selection reply / events / conflict / confirm / cleanup) and the persistence thread (`server/persist.rs`: `PersistMsg`, `spawn_persist_thread`). Code moved unchanged; `lister_msg` is left as the single message dispatch.
- Feature: quick apps in the panel. New `opusdm_ipc::quickapps` (`QuickApps` with `add`, `remove`, `move_to`, `quickapps.json` load / save; unit tested) and `panel/src/widgets/quickapps.rs`, a strip of icon-only launchers between the pager and the window list (hidden while empty). Left click launches, right click "Remove", drag onto another icon reorders. In the application menu a right click on an app shows "Add to quick apps" (disabled when already added). Every bar's strip is refreshed after any change.
- Fix: quick apps buttons use 3px horizontal padding (the generic panel button rule gave 8px) and no gap, so the icons sit close together.
- Removed the "Menu" tab from `opusdm-settings` (the `menu.json` editor moves to the upcoming "Edit Apps" tool). `desktop-config/src/menu_ui.rs` is kept, unused, to be reused there. The panel still reads `menu.json` unchanged.
- Feature: "Edit Apps" (desktop menu > Desktop), a KDE style menu editor, new crate `opusdm-apps-editor`. Tree of `menu.json` on the left (New Submenu, New Item, Up / Down, Delete, Reset, drag to move or to put into a folder), properties on the right (name, comment, command, icon with file chooser, work path, run in terminal). Nothing is written before Save; a close with unsaved changes asks. Editing a system app writes a same-id copy in `~/.local/share/applications/`; New Item creates `opusdm-<slug>.desktop`; only those own files are ever deleted. New pure `opusdm_ipc::desktop_entry` (`parse`, `render` keeping unrelated keys and groups, `slug`, `new_id`) and `menu::move_to` / `menu::children_len`, all unit tested. The panel's application menu re-reads `menu.json` each time it opens. Docs: `docs/usage.md` 3.30.
- Removed the unused `desktop-config/src/menu_ui.rs` (the old Settings Menu tab); the Apps editor replaced it.
- Fix: `scripts/build-release.sh` stages `opusdm-apps-editor` in `dist/bin` and `scripts/live-update.sh` knows its process name; it was missing, so "Edit Apps" could not start from an installed copy.
- Docs: design spec for the audio tray (volume, mute and default device for output and input in the panel, libpulse backend), `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-07-audio-tray-design.md`.
- Docs: implementation plan for the audio tray, `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-10-07-audio-tray.md`.
- Feature: audio tray in the panel. New crate `opusdm-audio` (GTK free): `model`, `volume` (percent <-> PulseAudio value, clamped at 100), `filter` (monitor sources are not inputs), `diff`, `error`, the `Backend` trait with `FakeBackend`, `PulseBackend` (one thread owns the libpulse threaded mainloop and context; other threads send it jobs) and `watch` (snapshot plus events, reconnects every 30 seconds and re-reports each outage). Panel: `audio_model.rs` (pure: list, icon level, tooltip, `DragGuard`, `coalesce`), `audio_center.rs` (shared state, one worker thread that merges queued volume requests) and `widgets/audio.rs` (speaker icon, popover with Output and Input sections). Tests: volume round trip and rounding, filter, diff, fake backend, row model, drag guard, coalescing; `cargo test -p opusdm-audio -- --ignored` runs the live check against the real server. Docs: `docs/usage.md` 3.32.
- Audio tray, verification status: built, clippy clean for the new code, unit tests and the live list test pass. NOT checked by hand in a session (the manual list of the plan): slider drag and no jump, mute, default switch, headset unplug, `pipewire-pulse` restart. The checks were skipped because a real `opusdm-hub` was running and a second hub in Xephyr could take over its socket.
- Fix (audio tray review): a request on a device that was just unplugged is reported as `NoSuchDevice` (libpulse errno `NoEntity`) and shows no error line; the panel connects with `NOAUTOSPAWN`, so it never starts a sound server. Tests: live `a_call_on_a_vanished_device_is_no_such_device`, `audio_model::an_error_about_a_vanished_device_is_not_shown`. The spec has an "As built" section.
- Feature: `opusdm-shot` capture delay. A "Delay (s)" spin button (0 to 60, default 0) in the bottom left of the editor, next to the format; the value is kept in `~/.config/opusdm/shot.json` (new `shot/src/config.rs`, unit tested) and applies from the next launch: the screen is captured after that many seconds. Not checked by hand in a session.
- Feature: "Extract archive" in the Lister right-click menu on a supported archive (`.zip`, `.7z`, `.tar`, `.tar.gz/bz2/xz/zst`, `.tgz`; not inside another archive). New job `Op::Extract` (`fsops/src/extract.rs`, system `unzip` / `tar` / `7z`, existing files are never overwritten, cancellable). It extracts into the DEST Lister; with no DEST a requester asks whether to extract into the current folder of this Lister (`lister/src/extract_dialog.rs`). Unit tested.
- Feature: OpusDM answers "show in folder". The hub serves `org.freedesktop.FileManager1` (`hub/src/file_manager.rs`: `ShowFolders`, `ShowItems`, `ShowItemProperties`, one Lister per distinct path) so the "Folder" button of a browser download opens a Lister instead of Dolphin. The bus name is never taken from a running file manager: if Dolphin's daemon already owns it the hub logs why and carries on. The Lister takes a file as its start argument too (`lister/src/start_target.rs`): it opens the parent folder with the file selected. New `assets/desktop/opusdm-lister.desktop` (`inode/directory`) calling `scripts/opusdm-open-folder` (asks the hub through `gdbus`); `build-release.sh` stages both and `install.sh` installs them. To make it the default: `xdg-mime default opusdm-lister.desktop inode/directory`. Unit tested (URI decoding, duplicates, start target). NOT checked by hand in a session: the D-Bus round trip with a real browser.
- Fix: the hub now queues for `org.freedesktop.FileManager1` instead of giving up when another file manager owns it, so it gets the name as soon as Dolphin's daemon (`plasma-dolphin.service`, started on demand by `xdg-desktop-portal`) exits. Live test: `dbus-run-session -- cargo test -p opusdm-hub show_items_on_the_bus -- --ignored`. The queue path itself (a second owner leaving) is NOT covered by a test.
- Feature: two new theme colours, `toolbar_button_bg` and `toolbar_button_fg` in `ColorSettings` (defaults `#2b2d31` / `#e3e3e3`, the dark chrome; older `theme.json` / `settings.json` files get them from `Default`). The Theme Editor's Colours tab has a "TOOLBAR BUTTON" row (background, icon colour) and every preset sets both. The Lister paints `button.opus-btn` with the background (instead of `@theme_bg_color`) and draws its SVG icons in the icon colour: `icons::svg_image` replaces `currentColor` with it, and `icons::set_icon_colour` (called by `update_css_for_colors`) re-tints the icons already on screen. Only `#rgb` / `#rrggbb` / `#rrggbbaa` values reach the SVG. Unit tested; NOT checked by hand in a session.
- Feature: archives are read with libarchive. New crate `opusdm-archive` (the only crate with `unsafe`; `libarchive3-sys` plus a safe API: `list`, `extract_entry`, `extract`, `is_archive_name`, `ArchiveError`). The format is detected from the content; the suffix list in `is_archive_name` replaces the two copies in the Lister and in fsops and now also accepts `.rar`, `.iso`, `.cpio` and `.tar.lz4`. Extraction uses the libarchive disk writer with `SECURE_NODOTDOT` and `SECURE_SYMLINKS`, never overwrites (an existing file is counted as kept, an existing folder is left as it is), drops setuid / setgid / sticky bits, polls the cancel callback between data blocks, matches names literally (no wildcard or option reading) and extracts a selected folder with everything below it. Encrypted archives fail with a clear error (no password prompt). Spec: `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-12-libarchive-backend-design.md`.
- Refactor: the Lister (`archive.rs`: listing, preview extraction, extraction of a selection), the preview pane listing (`preview.rs`) and the Extract job (`fsops/src/extract.rs`) call `opusdm-archive` instead of running `zipinfo`, `unzip`, `tar` and `7z`. Removed `ArchiveKind`, the three output parsers, `_unzip_pattern`, `_check_status` and the process polling of Extract. `7z` is still used to create archives (`compress.rs`). Archive listings now show the real permissions. Tests build their archives with the `tar` and `zip` crates, so they no longer skip when a tool is missing.
- Build requirement: `libarchive-dev` (pkg-config `libarchive`, for example `sudo apt install libarchive-dev`); `libarchive13` at run time.
- Fix: browsing an archive lists a folder once (an archive holding both the entry `sub/` and files below it showed `sub` twice) and shows the real dates of the entries instead of 1970-01-01: a folder takes the date of its own entry, else the newest date of its content. The row building is the pure `archive::_rows_in`, unit tested. The User and Group columns of archive rows are still a fixed uid / gid 1000, not the owner stored in the archive.
- Feature: a window a program asks to bring forward comes forward, even on another virtual desktop. Openbox refuses a `_NET_ACTIVE_WINDOW` request flagged as coming from an application for a window on another desktop (and whenever its focus stealing prevention says no) and only marks it as demanding attention: the browser tab opened for a login, or Telegram clicked in the tray while already open, stayed hidden somewhere. New `hub/src/activation_follow.rs`: a hub thread listens to those requests on the root window and, when the window has not become the active one 250 ms later, switches to its desktop (Openbox does not do it even for a pager request) and repeats the request as a pager, which Openbox honours (restore, raise, focus). Pager requests are never followed, so the hub cannot loop. X11 only. Unit tests for the decisions; live test `cargo test -p opusdm-hub --lib activation_follow -- --ignored` passed in Xephyr with Openbox and `openbox-rc.xml`. NOT checked by hand in the real session (needs a hub restart): Claude Code login in the browser, Telegram from the tray.
- Fix: dragging files out of an archive shown in a Lister onto another Lister failed with "IO error ... No such file or directory": the drag carried the virtual path (`/x/a.zip#dir/file`), which the job runner cannot read. The drop target (column view and icon field) now extracts those entries first, off the GTK thread, into a scratch folder (one sub folder per archive) kept until the job ends, then runs the job on the extracted copies; a Move runs as a Copy because an archive is read-only (`lister/src/dnd.rs`, `submit_dropped`). Unit tested (grouping per archive); NOT checked by hand with a real drag.
- Fix: dropping a file from an archive on a Lister failed with "extraction failed: /tmp/opusdm-extract-XXXX/0 is not a folder": `opusdm_archive::extract` needs an existing folder and the per archive sub folder made by `submit_dropped` was never created. `archive::extract_items_to_dir` now creates its destination folder. Unit tested (`extract_items_creates_the_destination_folder`).
- Release: version bumped to 0.8.0 (workspace `Cargo.toml`, so every crate, `opusdm_ui::VERSION` and `Cargo.lock`).
- Fix: "Copy As..." / "Move As..." on files inside an archive failed with "IO error ... No such file or directory": the job got the virtual path (`a.zip#dir/file`). Both dialogs (single and batch) now go through `actions::submit_selection`, which extracts the selection first like the plain Copy button (`transfer_from_archive`, now public); `MoveAs` runs as `CopyAs` because an archive is read-only. NOT checked by hand with a real archive.
- Feature: right click on an empty area of a Lister (below the rows in the column view, or between icons in the icon view) shows a menu with "Make Directory..." and "New File...", the same commands as `Ctrl+N` / `Ctrl+Shift+N` (`context_menu::show_empty_area_menu`, `attach_empty_area`). Rows and the column header keep their own behaviour. Unit tested (`is_empty_area`, command list); NOT checked by hand in a session.
- Feature: "Open in Viewer" in the Lister right-click menu of files and folders. It starts `opusdm-viewer` with the selection (or the clicked row when it is not selected) as arguments, in order (`context_menu::open_in_viewer`, reusing `image_viewer::launch`). Entries inside an archive or on a remote server are refused with a message, since the viewer reads local paths only (`viewer_targets`, unit tested). NOT checked by hand in a session.
- Feature: details popups for the panel CPU and network monitors. A left click on a meter opens a borderless popup (popover without arrow) below it; it closes on a click outside. CPU: total and a larger 60 second graph, a bar and percentage per core, load average with running / total tasks, the five busiest processes (share of the whole machine; processes are read only while the popup is open). Network: totals and a larger graph, a row per interface (loopback excluded) with download / upload rate and totals since the interface came up, and the peak rates of the last minute. New pure `panel/src/sysmon_model.rs` (the `/proc` parsers moved there from `widgets/sysmon.rs`, plus per core, `/proc/loadavg`, per interface and `/proc/<pid>/stat` parsing and the busiest list), unit tested. NOT checked by hand in a session (needs a hub restart).
- Feature: right click on the panel audio icon switches the sound off and on for the whole system: it toggles the mute of the default output (`audio_model::Model::toggle_output_mute`, `AudioCenter::toggle_output_mute`). The icon changes to the crossed speaker when the server reports the mute. With no output device a short message is shown. Unit tested; docs `docs/usage.md` 3.32. NOT checked by hand in a session (needs a hub restart).
- Fix: a notification popup no longer steals the keyboard focus from the active window on X11 (Openbox focuses new windows). `opusdm-notify` now sets `_NET_WM_USER_TIME` to 0, `WM_HINTS` input to false and drops `WM_TAKE_FOCUS` from `WM_PROTOCOLS` on its popups (`popup.rs`, `refuse_x11_input_focus`). Checked in Xephyr with Openbox and `openbox-rc.xml` (`notify-send` leaves the active window unchanged); popup clicks need no focus. Wayland was already `KeyboardMode::None`.
- Fix: removed the unused `gtk4::prelude::*` import in `ui/src/app_icon.rs` (build warning).
- Feature: "Open with..." in the Lister right-click menu of files and folders. It opens a window (`lister/src/open_with.rs`) listing the applications registered for the type of the clicked file (name guessed from the file name; icon and description when available), and at the bottom an input box with the same suggestions as the Run window. Double click on a handler, Enter in the box (Up / Down pick a suggestion) or the Open button start the application on the whole selection; a failure is logged and flashed in the Lister. Refactor: the Run matching (`search.rs`) and the desktop entry builder (`app_entry`, `installed_entries`) moved from `opusdm-run` to `opusdm-apps` so both share them; `opusdm-run` behaviour is unchanged (its tests pass). New `opusdm_apps::apps::open_files`. Build, clippy and unit tests pass. NOT checked by hand in a session (Lister window, Run window after the move, handler launch): needs `scripts/live-update.sh` or `run-x11.sh`.
- Config: removed the Alt+wheel and Ctrl+Alt+wheel (Frame, Desktop and Root contexts) desktop switch mouse bindings from `openbox-rc.xml` (Alt+Shift+wheel, send window to desktop, is kept). Needs `openbox --reconfigure`; NOT checked in a session.
- Refactor: `opusdm-lister` is now a library plus the binary (`crates/lister/src/lib.rs` holds the `pub mod` list and the `dlog!` macro, `main.rs` keeps `main` and the tool runners), so `opusdm-settings` can embed the Hotkeys and File Types editors. Code moved unchanged. New cargo feature `terminal` (default on) gates `vte4`: without it `terminal_stub.rs` gives `TerminalDrawer` the same API with no shell (`toggle` logs). Ownership and event flow are untouched. Build with and without the feature, the 425 unit tests and `cargo tree --no-default-features` (no `vte`) checked. NOT checked by hand in a session (Lister: Enter in the path bar, double click, Backspace, Alt+Left/Right, F-keys, F4 terminal; global hotkeys; hub roles): a real hub runs in the session and no input driver is available here, so it needs `scripts/live-update.sh --all` or `run-x11.sh` by the owner.
- Feature: the Advanced tab of `opusdm-settings` is a list of tools on the left (Hotkeys, File Types, Input, Network) and the picked tool on the right (`theme-config/src/advanced.rs`); under the list the buttons of the tools that keep their own window (Manage Functions, Scripts, Button Banks, Apps, Screens). The System tab is gone. New `opusdm_ui::EmbeddedTool` (title, widget, is_dirty, save, revert) and `confirm_leave`: leaving a tool with unsaved changes (another entry, closing Settings) asks Save / Discard / Cancel (Cancel is an extra choice, easy to drop); Save applies and leaves only if it worked, Discard reverts, Cancel stays. A tool is built on first pick and kept. The Settings window is wider (820 px). The four entries are placeholders with an "Open ..." button until each tool is embedded, one commit per tool. Unit tested: the leave decision. NOT checked by hand in a session (layout, entry switch, close with a dirty tool).
- Feature: the Hotkeys editor is embedded in the Advanced tab of `opusdm-settings` (`opusdm_lister::hotkey_editor::embedded`, an `EmbeddedTool`). It loads the hub's copy over a tool connection, has its own Save and Revert buttons and a status line, and is dirty while the rows differ from the saved hotkeys. Save asks the hub for its config again right before sending, so the Menu and Scripts sections changed meanwhile by other tools are not overwritten with a stale copy (tool connections get no broadcasts). A hub that cannot be reached or quit shows as a message / a failed save, logged with `err_log!`. The standalone `opusdm-lister --edit-hotkeys` window keeps working: `build_editor` shares the new `build_body` with it. `opusdm-settings` depends on `opusdm-lister` with `default-features = false`, so it does not link VTE (`cargo tree -p opusdm-theme-config` has no `vte`). Build, clippy and unit tests pass. NOT checked by hand in a session (no GTK test harness in the Lister, no input driver here): load, edit, Save against a running hub, Revert, switching entry with unsaved rows (Save / Discard / Cancel), close Settings with unsaved rows, standalone `--edit-hotkeys`.
- Feature: the File Types editor is embedded in the Advanced tab (`opusdm_lister::filetypes_ui::embedded`, an `EmbeddedTool`), with its own Save and Revert. It is dirty while the drafts differ from the last saved ones (`Draft` is `PartialEq`); Save writes `filetypes.json` and drops the in-memory cache like the standalone window; problems in the drafts or a write error stay in the status line, are logged for a write error, and keep the tool dirty (a failed save never lets you leave silently). The Functions come from the hub, from disk when it does not answer (logged). The Settings window default width is now 1060 px so the editor has room. The standalone `--edit-filetypes` window and the Lister's own editor share the new `build_body`; their Save keeps its messages (`SaveError`). Build, clippy and the 425 Lister tests pass. NOT checked by hand in a session: load, edit, Save, Revert, switch entry with unsaved edits (Save / Discard / Cancel), standalone `--edit-filetypes`, the Lister's File Types dialog (right click > Create filetype from a file).
- Feature: the Input tabs (Mouse, Touchpad, Keyboard) are embedded in the Advanced tab (`opusdm_input_config::ui::embedded`, an `EmbeddedTool`). Changes still apply live; Save keeps them and the page stays open; Revert (and Discard in the question when leaving) puts the saved values back and rebuilds the tabs from them. The `ui` module moved from the binary to the library (`opusdm-input-config` has GTK in its library now, the hub already links GTK); `opusdm-input-config` itself runs the same code in its own window (`Mode::Window`: closes after Save and after a revert, as before).
- Behaviour change in `input_config::session::Session::commit`: the saved settings become the snapshot a later revert goes back to. Only a window that stays open after Save can see it (the Input window closes after Save), so the standalone window is unchanged. Unit test renamed `a_change_after_a_save_is_reverted_to_the_saved_values`.
- Contract change: `EmbeddedTool::revert(done)` takes a completion callback. Input undoes its live changes on a worker thread, and a host that closes right after Discard would end the process before the revert ran; `confirm_leave` now proceeds only after `done`. Hotkeys and File Types call it at once.
- Input embedded, verification status: build, clippy and the 63 unit tests (session, worker) pass. NOT checked by hand in a session: live apply of the sliders and checks, Save, Revert (the system really goes back, tabs rebuilt), switch entry with live changes (Save / Discard / Cancel), close Settings with live changes (the revert must finish before the window goes), the standalone `opusdm-input-config` (Save closes, close reverts, Escape).
- Feature: the Network connections are embedded in the Advanced tab (`opusdm_network_config::window::embedded`, an `EmbeddedTool`): the same list and editor as `opusdm-network-manager`, with their own Save, Revert, Connect and message line. The GUI modules (`window`, `editor`, `form`, `pages`, `vpn_pages`, `dialogs`) and `APP_CSS` moved from the binary to the library; `App.window` is a `gtk::Window` (the program's window or the host's); the program's close handler moved to `App::guard_close`, used only by `open`, so the standalone window closes exactly as before (same handler order: list wiring, `guard_close`, `keep_until_closed`). In a host the host asks: Save sends the save and answers through `save_done` when the worker reports `Saved` or a failed save (a save that cannot be sent, or a connection with errors, answers `false` and shows why); Discard reverts the editor, or drops a connection never saved (it would stay dirty forever). The embedded tool has its own secret agent identifier (`SETTINGS_IDENTIFIER`, `org.opusdm.network.settings`) so it does not clash with the Network program, and installs the form styles once.
- Settings: the placeholders of the Advanced tab are gone, all four entries are embedded tools. Docs: `docs/usage.md` 3.28b and the three "open it" notes.
- Network embedded, verification status: workspace build, clippy and the unit tests of the touched crates pass (`CloseState` and the worker are unchanged). NOT checked by hand in a session: list and editor inside Settings, Save then switch entry, Discard of a new connection, a failed save keeping you on the entry, close Settings with an unsaved connection, secret agent registration next to a running Network program, the standalone `opusdm-network-manager` (close with unsaved changes asks as before), the panel "Configure connections..." button.
- Not checked in any session for the whole Advanced tab (rule 4 of CLAUDE.md): the Lister (Enter in the path bar, double click, Backspace, Alt+Left/Right, F-keys, F4 terminal), the global hotkeys (Ctrl+backslash, Ctrl+Shift+space, Ctrl+Print, Alt+1/2), the hub (second Lister, SOURCE/DEST, Copy job, function run). The `opusdm-lister` library split touched every Lister module path, so these need one run by the owner (`scripts/live-update.sh --all`, or `run-x11.sh`).
- Settings: the Cancel / Apply row is hidden on the Advanced tab only (`TABS_WITHOUT_BUTTONS`, `notebook.connect_switch_page` in `theme-config/src/main.rs`); its tools have their own Save and Revert. Dock and Startup Apps keep the row because Apply writes their values. Build and tests pass; NOT checked by hand in a session.
- Release: version bumped to 0.11.0 (workspace `Cargo.toml`, so every crate, `opusdm_ui::VERSION` and `Cargo.lock`). Includes the Advanced tab of `opusdm-settings` with the embedded Hotkeys, File Types, Input and Network tools.
- Fix (lister, run): "Open with..." killed the Lister before its window appeared. `installed_entries()` called `DesktopAppInfo::executable()` on every installed app, and an entry without `Exec=` (here a hand-made `~/.local/share/applications/IsaacSim.desktop`) makes GIO return NULL: the binding panics (`assertion failed: !ptr.is_null()` in glib `translate.rs`), which in the release build aborted as `segfault at 0` in `strlen` (seen twice in the kernel log). New `program_name()` in `crates/apps/src/entries.rs` asks only when the key `Exec` exists; an entry without it is kept in the search, without the program name in its keywords, and logged with `err_log!("apps", "<id>: desktop entry without Exec=")`. The Run window shares `installed_entries()`, so it had the same crash. Ownership and lifetimes are untouched. Tests: `an_entry_without_exec_has_no_program_and_does_not_panic`, `an_entry_with_exec_reports_its_program`. Checked in Xephyr with the release build: right click on a file, "Open with..." shows the window (Okular, WOW3 Presentation, Ark), typing "isaac" suggests "Isaac Sim", Escape closes it and the Lister stays alive; the same click crashed the Lister before the fix. NOT checked: launching an app from the dialog (Open button / Enter), the Run window (Ctrl+Shift+space), Enter in the path bar, double click on a folder, Backspace, Alt+Left/Right, F-keys, the other global hotkeys, the hub roles and Copy jobs, the real desktop session (needs `scripts/live-update.sh --all`).
